/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1158 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1159 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1160 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1161 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {
1162 |       if (lastVarArgs && i == max) {
1163 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1164 |       } else {
1165 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1166 |       }
1167 |     }
1168 |     return builder.build();
1169 |   }

===== M0005 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 422-466
Documentation:

Method code:
422 |   @Override
423 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
424 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
425 |       return true;
426 |     }
427 | 
428 |     // Union types
429 |     if (that instanceof UnionType) {
430 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
431 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
432 |       return false;
433 |     }
434 | 
435 |     // record types
436 |     if (that instanceof RecordType) {
437 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, (RecordType) that);
438 |     }
439 | 
440 |     // Interfaces
441 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
442 |     // to the interface instance.
443 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
444 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
445 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
446 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
447 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
448 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
449 |           return true;
450 |         }
451 |       }
452 |     }
453 | 
454 |     // other prototype based objects
455 |     if (that != null) {
456 |       if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
457 |         // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
458 |         // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
459 |         // to avoid guessing.
460 |         return true;
461 |       }
462 |       return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
463 |     }
464 | 
465 |     return false;
466 |   }

===== M0008 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 196-215
Documentation:
/**
   * Given an entry node, find all the nodes reachable from that node
   * and prioritize them.
   */
Method code:
196 |   private void prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry) {
197 |     PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> worklist =
198 |         new PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>>(10, priorityComparator);
199 |     worklist.add(entry);
200 | 
201 |     while (!worklist.isEmpty()) {
202 |       DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> current = worklist.remove();
203 |       if (nodePriorities.containsKey(current)) {
204 |         continue;
205 |       }
206 | 
207 |       nodePriorities.put(current, ++priorityCounter);
208 | 
209 |       List<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> successors =
210 |           cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(current);
211 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : successors) {
212 |         worklist.add(candidate);
213 |       }
214 |     }
215 |   }

609 |   private static String toSource(Node root, Format outputFormat,
610 |                                  boolean lineBreak,  int lineLengthThreshold,
611 |                                  SourceMap sourceMap,
612 |                                  SourceMap.DetailLevel sourceMapDetailLevel,
613 |                                  Charset outputCharset,
614 |                                  boolean tagAsStrict) {

624 |             lineLengthThreshold, createSourceMap, sourceMapDetailLevel);

626 |         outputFormat == Format.TYPED

===== M0023 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 734-776
Documentation:
/**
   * A function is a subtype of another if their call methods are related via
   * subtyping and {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that} with regard to
   * the prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
734 |   @Override
735 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
736 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
737 |       return true;
738 |     }
739 | 
740 |     if (that.isFunctionType()) {
741 |       if (((FunctionType) that).isInterface()) {
742 |         // Any function can be assigned to an interface function.
743 |         return true;
744 |       }
745 |       if (this.isInterface()) {
746 |         // An interface function cannot be assigned to anything.
747 |         return false;
748 |       }
749 |       // If functionA is a subtype of functionB, then their "this" types
750 |       // should be contravariant. However, this causes problems because
751 |       // of the way we enforce overrides. Because function(this:SubFoo)
752 |       // is not a subtype of function(this:Foo), our override check treats
753 |       // this as an error. It also screws up out standard method
754 |       // for aliasing constructors. Let's punt on all this for now.
755 |       // TODO(nicksantos): fix this.
756 |       FunctionType other = (FunctionType) that;
757 |       boolean treatThisTypesAsCovariant =
758 |         // If either one of these is a ctor, skip 'this' checking.
759 |         this.isConstructor() || other.isConstructor() ||
760 | 
761 |         // An interface 'this'-type is non-restrictive.
762 |         // In practical terms, if C implements I, and I has a method m,
763 |         // then any m doesn't necessarily have to C#m's 'this'
764 |         // type doesn't need to match I.
765 |         (other.typeOfThis.getConstructor() != null &&
766 |              other.typeOfThis.getConstructor().isInterface()) ||
767 | 
768 |         // If one of the 'this' types is covariant of the other,
769 |         // then we'll treat them as covariant (see comment above).
770 |         other.typeOfThis.isSubtype(this.typeOfThis) ||
771 |         this.typeOfThis.isSubtype(other.typeOfThis);
772 |       return treatThisTypesAsCovariant && this.call.isSubtype(other.call);
773 |     }
774 | 
775 |     return getNativeType(JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_PROTOTYPE).isSubtype(that);
776 |   }

===== M0024 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleIf(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 357-370
Documentation:

Method code:
357 |   private void handleIf(Node node) {
358 |     Node thenBlock = node.getFirstChild().getNext();
359 |     Node elseBlock = thenBlock.getNext();
360 |     createEdge(node, Branch.ON_TRUE, computeFallThrough(thenBlock));
361 | 
362 |     if (elseBlock == null) {
363 |       createEdge(node, Branch.ON_FALSE,
364 |           computeFollowNode(node, this)); // not taken branch
365 |     } else {
366 |       createEdge(node, Branch.ON_FALSE, computeFallThrough(elseBlock));
367 |     }
368 |     connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(
369 |         node, NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(node));
370 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType)

Documentation:
/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
   * @return {@code true} if {@code prototype} is {@code equal} to any
   *         object in this object's implicit prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
447 |   final boolean isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType prototype) {
448 |     for (ObjectType current = this;
449 |          current != null;
450 |          current = current.getImplicitPrototype()) {
451 |       if (current.isEquivalentTo(prototype)) {
452 |         return true;
453 |       }
454 |     }
455 |     return false;
456 |   }

===== M0052 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleReturn(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 622-649
Documentation:

Method code:
622 |   private void handleReturn(Node node) {
623 |     Node lastJump = null;
624 |     for (Iterator<Node> iter = exceptionHandler.iterator(); iter.hasNext();) {
625 |       Node curHandler = iter.next();
626 |       if (NodeUtil.isFunction(curHandler)) {
627 |         break;
628 |       }
629 |       if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(curHandler)) {
630 |         if (lastJump == null) {
631 |           createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, curHandler.getLastChild());
632 |         } else {
633 |           finallyMap.put(lastJump,
634 |               computeFallThrough(curHandler.getLastChild()));
635 |         }
636 |         lastJump = curHandler;
637 |       }
638 |     }
639 | 
640 |     if (node.hasChildren()) {
641 |       connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(node, node.getFirstChild());
642 |     }
643 | 
644 |     if (lastJump == null) {
645 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, null);
646 |     } else {
647 |       finallyMap.put(lastJump, null);
648 |     }
649 |   }

===== M0057 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 221-273
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
221 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
222 |     this.options = options;
223 |     if (errorManager == null) {
224 |       if (outStream == null) {
225 |         setErrorManager(
226 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
227 |       } else {
228 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
229 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
230 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
231 |         setErrorManager(printer);
232 |       }
233 |     }
234 | 
235 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
236 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
237 |     guards.add(
238 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
239 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
240 |     WarningsGuard warningsGuard = options.getWarningsGuard();
241 |     if (warningsGuard != null) {
242 |       guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
243 |     }
244 | 
245 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
246 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
247 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
248 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
249 |     // error off.
250 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
251 |         (warningsGuard == null || !warningsGuard.disables(
252 |             DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES))) {
253 |       guards.add(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
254 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
255 |     }
256 | 
257 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
258 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
259 |       options.checkTypes = true;
260 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
261 |       options.checkTypes = false;
262 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
263 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
264 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
265 |       // parser type warnings.
266 |       guards.add(
267 |           new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
268 |               DiagnosticGroup.forType(
269 |                   RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
270 |               CheckLevel.OFF));
271 |     }
272 |     this.warningsGuard = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
273 |   }

===== M0067 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1146-1154
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1146 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1147 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1148 |       return -1;
1149 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1150 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1151 |     } else {
1152 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1153 |     }
1154 |   }

===== M0068 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 888-915
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
888 |   static boolean isSubtype(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
889 |     // unknown
890 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
891 |       return true;
892 |     }
893 |     // equality
894 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
895 |       return true;
896 |     }
897 |     // all type
898 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
899 |       return true;
900 |     }
901 |     // unions
902 |     if (thatType instanceof UnionType) {
903 |       UnionType union = (UnionType)thatType;
904 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
905 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
906 |           return true;
907 |         }
908 |       }
909 |     }
910 |     // named types
911 |     if (thatType instanceof NamedType) {
912 |       return thisType.isSubtype(((NamedType)thatType).getReferencedType());
913 |     }
914 |     return false;
915 |   }

===== M0069 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 437-465
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
437 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
438 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
439 |     int type = n.getType();
440 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
441 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
442 |     }
443 | 
444 |     curNode = n;
445 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
446 | 
447 |     switch (type) {
448 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
449 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
450 |         break;
451 | 
452 |       default:
453 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
454 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
455 |           // would no longer point to the true next
456 |           Node next = child.getNext();
457 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
458 |           child = next;
459 |         }
460 |         break;
461 |     }
462 | 
463 |     curNode = n;
464 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
465 |   }

===== M0077 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.addNonEmptyStatement(Node,Context,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 737-793
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds a block or expression, substituting a VOID with an empty statement.
   * This is used for "for (...);" and "if (...);" type statements.
   *
   * @param n The node to print.
   * @param context The context to determine how the node should be printed.
   */
Method code:
737 |   private void addNonEmptyStatement(
738 |       Node n, Context context, boolean allowNonBlockChild) {
739 |     Node nodeToProcess = n;
740 | 
741 |     if (!allowNonBlockChild && n.getType() != Token.BLOCK) {
742 |       throw new Error("Missing BLOCK child.");
743 |     }
744 | 
745 |     // Strip unneeded blocks, that is blocks with <2 children unless
746 |     // the CodePrinter specifically wants to keep them.
747 |     if (n.getType() == Token.BLOCK) {
748 |       int count = getNonEmptyChildCount(n, 2);
749 |       if (count == 0) {
750 |         if (cc.shouldPreserveExtraBlocks()) {
751 |           cc.beginBlock();
752 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
753 |         } else {
754 |           cc.endStatement(true);
755 |         }
756 |         return;
757 |       }
758 | 
759 |       if (count == 1) {
760 |         // Hack around a couple of browser bugs:
761 |         //   Safari needs a block around function declarations.
762 |         //   IE6/7 needs a block around DOs.
763 |         Node firstAndOnlyChild = getFirstNonEmptyChild(n);
764 |         boolean alwaysWrapInBlock = cc.shouldPreserveExtraBlocks();
765 |         if (alwaysWrapInBlock || isOneExactlyFunctionOrDo(firstAndOnlyChild)) {
766 |           cc.beginBlock();
767 |           add(firstAndOnlyChild, Context.STATEMENT);
768 |           cc.maybeLineBreak();
769 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
770 |           return;
771 |         } else {
772 |           // Continue with the only child.
773 |           nodeToProcess = firstAndOnlyChild;
774 |         }
775 |       }
776 | 
777 |       if (count > 1) {
778 |         context = Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
779 |       }
780 |     }
781 | 
782 |     if (nodeToProcess.getType() == Token.EMPTY) {
783 |       cc.endStatement(true);
784 |     } else {
785 |       add(nodeToProcess, context);
786 | 
787 |       // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions - so any
788 |       // VAR in a statement context needs a call to endStatement() here.
789 |       if (nodeToProcess.getType() == Token.VAR) {
790 |         cc.endStatement();
791 |       }
792 |     }
793 |   }

===== M0085 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 813-840
Documentation:
/**
   * Connects cfgNode to the proper CATCH block if target subtree might throw
   * an exception. If there are FINALLY blocks reached before a CATCH, it will
   * make the corresponding entry in finallyMap.
   */
Method code:
813 |   private void connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node cfgNode, Node target) {
814 |     if (mayThrowException(target) && !exceptionHandler.isEmpty()) {
815 |       Node lastJump = cfgNode;
816 |       for (Node handler : exceptionHandler) {
817 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunction(handler)) {
818 |           return;
819 |         }
820 |         Preconditions.checkState(handler.getType() == Token.TRY);
821 |         Node catchBlock = NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(handler);
822 | 
823 |         if (!NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(catchBlock)) { // No catch but a FINALLY.
824 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
825 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, handler.getLastChild());
826 |           } else {
827 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, handler.getLastChild());
828 |           }
829 |         } else { // Has a catch.
830 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
831 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, catchBlock);
832 |             return;
833 |           } else {
834 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, catchBlock);
835 |           }
836 |         }
837 |         lastJump = handler;
838 |       }
839 |     }
840 |   }

===== M0090 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addOp(String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 203-231
Documentation:

Method code:
203 |   void addOp(String op, boolean binOp) {
204 |     maybeEndStatement();
205 | 
206 |     char first = op.charAt(0);
207 |     char prev = getLastChar();
208 | 
209 |     if ((first == '+' || first == '-') && prev == first) {
210 |       // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of
211 |       // things like "x + ++y" or "x++ + ++y"
212 |       append(" ");
213 |     } else if (Character.isLetter(first) &&
214 |                isWordChar(prev)) {
215 |       // Make sure there is a space after e.g. instanceof , typeof
216 |       append(" ");
217 |     } else if (prev == '-' && first == '>') {
218 |       // Make sure that we don't emit -->
219 |       append(" ");
220 |     }
221 | 
222 |     // Allow formating around the operator.
223 |     appendOp(op, binOp);
224 | 
225 |     // Line breaking after an operator is always safe. Line breaking before an
226 |     // operator on the other hand is not. We only line break after a bin op
227 |     // because it looks strange.
228 |     if (binOp) {
229 |       maybeCutLine();
230 |     }
231 |   }

===== M0093 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseAtScope(Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 320-338
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a parse tree recursively with a scope, starting at that scope's
   * root.
   */
Method code:
320 |   void traverseAtScope(Scope s) {
321 |     Node n = s.getRootNode();
322 |     if (n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
323 |       // We need to do some extra magic to make sure that the scope doesn't
324 |       // get re-created when we dive into the function.
325 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
326 |       curNode = n;
327 |       pushScope(s);
328 | 
329 |       Node args = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
330 |       Node body = args.getNext();
331 |       traverseBranch(args, n);
332 |       traverseBranch(body, n);
333 | 
334 |       popScope();
335 |     } else {
336 |       traverseWithScope(n, s);
337 |     }
338 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initModules(List<JSSourceFile>,List<JSModule>,CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 311-342
Documentation:
/**
   * Initializes the instance state needed for a compile job if the sources
   * are in modules.
   */
Method code:
311 |   public void initModules(
312 |       List<JSSourceFile> externs, List<JSModule> modules,
313 |       CompilerOptions options) {
314 |     initOptions(options);
315 | 
316 |     checkFirstModule(modules);
317 |     fillEmptyModules(modules);

327 |       } catch (JSModuleGraph.ModuleDependenceException e) {

336 |     }
337 |

191 |       case Token.COLON:
192 |         return processObjectProperty((ObjectProperty) node);
193 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
194 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
195 |       case Token.DO:
196 |         return processDoLoop((DoLoop) node);
197 |       case Token.EMPTY:
198 |         return processEmptyExpression((EmptyExpression) node);
199 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
200 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
201 |         if (node instanceof ExpressionStatement) {
202 |           return processExpressionStatement((ExpressionStatement) node);
203 |         } else  if (node instanceof LabeledStatement) {
204 |           return processLabeledStatement((LabeledStatement) node);
205 |         } else {

219 |           return processForInLoop((ForInLoop) node);

221 |           return processForLoop((ForLoop) node);

226 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
227 |         }
228 |       case Token.FUNCTION:

230 |       case Token.GETELEM:
231 |         return processElementGet((ElementGet) node);
232 |       case Token.GETPROP:

246 |       case Token.NUMBER:

250 |       case Token.REGEXP:

252 |       case Token.RETURN:

254 |       case Token.SCRIPT:

256 |       case Token.STRING:
257 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
258 |       case Token.SWITCH:
259 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
260 |       case Token.THROW:
261 |         return processThrowStatement((ThrowStatement) node);
262 |       case Token.TRY:

277 |         return processWhileLoop((WhileLoop) node);
278 |       case Token.WITH:

280 |     }

===== M0117 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanRoot(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 82-112
Documentation:

Method code:
82 |   private void scanRoot(Node n, Scope parent) {
83 |     if (n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
84 |       sourceName = (String) n.getProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP);
85 | 
86 |       final Node fnNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
87 |       final Node args = fnNameNode.getNext();
88 |       final Node body = args.getNext();
89 | 
90 |       // Bleed the function name into the scope, if it hasn't
91 |       // been declared in the outer scope.
92 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
93 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty() && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
94 |         declareVar(fnNameNode);
95 |       }
96 | 
97 |       // Args: Declare function variables
98 |       Preconditions.checkState(args.getType() == Token.LP);
99 |       for (Node a = args.getFirstChild(); a != null;
100 |            a = a.getNext()) {
101 |         Preconditions.checkState(a.getType() == Token.NAME);
102 |         declareVar(a);
103 |       }
104 | 
105 |       // Body
106 |       scanVars(body, n);
107 |     } else {
108 |       // It's the global block
109 |       Preconditions.checkState(scope.getParent() == null);
110 |       scanVars(n, null);
111 |     }
112 |   }

===== M0118 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.getNonEmptyChildCount(Node,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1054-1065
Documentation:
/**
   * @param maxCount The maximum number of children to look for.
   * @return The number of children of this node that are non empty up to
   * maxCount.
   */
Method code:
1054 |   private static int getNonEmptyChildCount(Node n, int maxCount) {
1055 |     int i = 0;
1056 |     Node c = n.getFirstChild();
1057 |     for (; c != null && i < maxCount; c = c.getNext()) {
1058 |       if (c.getType() == Token.BLOCK) {
1059 |         i += getNonEmptyChildCount(c, maxCount-i);
1060 |       } else if (c.getType() != Token.EMPTY) {
1061 |         i++;
1062 |       }
1063 |     }
1064 |     return i;
1065 |   }

/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or

   */
Method code:
775 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
776 |     if (item == null) {
777 |       return null;
778 |     } else if (item.type == propType) {
779 |       return item.next;
780 |     } else {
781 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.next, propType);
782 |       if (result != item.next) {
783 |         return new PropListItem(
784 |             item.type, item.intValue, item.objectValue, result);
785 |       } else {
786 |         return item;
787 |       }
788 |     }
789 |   }

===== M0162 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1028-1048
Documentation:

Method code:
1028 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
1029 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
1030 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
1031 |       return s;
1032 |     }
1033 | 
1034 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-latin characters if needed.
1035 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
1036 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1037 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1038 |       // Identifiers should always go to Latin1/ ASCII characters because
1039 |       // different browser's rules for valid identifier characters are
1040 |       // crazy.
1041 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
1042 |         sb.append(c);
1043 |       } else {
1044 |         appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1045 |       }
1046 |     }
1047 |     return sb.toString();
1048 |   }

===== M0163 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.maybeLineBreak()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 433-453
Documentation:

Method code:
433 |     @Override
434 |     void maybeLineBreak() {
435 |       if (lineBreak) {
436 |         if (sawFunction) {
437 |           startNewLine();
438 |           sawFunction = false;
439 |         }
440 |       }
441 | 
442 |       // Since we are at a legal line break, can we upgrade the
443 |       // preferred break position?  We prefer to break after a
444 |       // semicolon rather than before it.
445 |       int len = code.length();
446 |       if (preferredBreakPosition == len - 1) {
447 |         char ch = code.charAt(len - 1);
448 |         if (ch == ';') {
449 |           preferredBreakPosition = len;
450 |         }
451 |       }
452 |       maybeCutLine();
453 |     }

===== M0165 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DeadAssignmentsElimination.checkHookBranchReadBeforeKill(Node,Node,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DeadAssignmentsElimination.java
Original range: 384-400
Documentation:

Method code:
384 |   private VariableLiveness checkHookBranchReadBeforeKill(
385 |       Node trueCase, Node falseCase, String variable) {
386 |     VariableLiveness v1 = isVariableReadBeforeKill(
387 |       trueCase, variable);
388 |     VariableLiveness v2 = isVariableReadBeforeKill(
389 |       falseCase, variable);
390 |     // With a hook it is unknown which branch will run, so
391 |     // we must be conservative.  A read by either is a READ, and
392 |     // a KILL is only considered if both KILL.
393 |     if (v1 == VariableLiveness.READ || v2 == VariableLiveness.READ) {
394 |       return VariableLiveness.READ;
395 |     } else if (v1 == VariableLiveness.KILL && v2 == VariableLiveness.KILL) {
396 |       return VariableLiveness.KILL;
397 |     } else {
398 |       return VariableLiveness.MAYBE_LIVE;
399 |     }
400 |   }

Original range: 189-203
Documentation:

Method code:
189 |   private Node transformBlock(AstNode node) {
190 |     Node irNode = transform(node);
191 |     if (irNode.getType() != Token.BLOCK) {
192 |       if (irNode.getType() == Token.EMPTY) {
193 |         irNode.setType(Token.BLOCK);
194 |         irNode.setWasEmptyNode(true);
195 |       } else {
196 |         Node newBlock = newNode(Token.BLOCK, irNode);
197 |         newBlock.setLineno(irNode.getLineno());
198 |         newBlock.setCharno(irNode.getCharno());
199 |         irNode = newBlock;
200 |       }
201 |     }
202 |     return irNode;
203 |   }

611 |     @Override

===== M0181 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowGraph.java
Original range: 150-195
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if n should be represented by a new CFG node in the control
   * flow graph.
   */
Method code:
150 |   public static boolean isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node n) {
151 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
152 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
153 |       case Token.BLOCK:
154 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
155 |       case Token.TRY:
156 |       case Token.FINALLY:
157 |         return true;
158 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
159 |         // A function node represents the start of a function where the name
160 |         // is bleed into the local scope and parameters has been assigned
161 |         // to the formal argument names. The node includes the name of the
162 |         // function and the LP list since we assume the whole set up process
163 |         // is atomic without change in control flow. The next change of
164 |         // control is going into the function's body represent by the second
165 |         // child.
166 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
167 |       case Token.WHILE:
168 |       case Token.DO:
169 |       case Token.IF:
170 |         // Theses control structure is represented by its node that holds the
171 |         // condition. Each of them is a branch node based on its condition.
172 |         return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
173 | 
174 |       case Token.FOR:
175 |         // The FOR(;;) node differs from other control structure in that
176 |         // it has a initialization and a increment statement. Those
177 |         // two statements have its corresponding CFG nodes to represent them.
178 |         // The FOR node represents the condition check for each iteration.
179 |         // That way the following:
180 |         // for(var x = 0; x < 10; x++) { } has a graph that is isomorphic to
181 |         // var x = 0; while(x<10) {  x++; }
182 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
183 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
184 |         } else {
185 |           return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
186 |         }
187 |       case Token.SWITCH:
188 |       case Token.CASE:
189 |       case Token.CATCH:
190 |       case Token.WITH:
191 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild();
192 |       default:
193 |         return false;
194 |     }
195 |   }

===== M0185 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 95-156
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
95 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
96 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
97 |     // just bail out early here.
98 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
99 |       return this;
100 |     }
101 | 
102 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
103 | 
104 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
105 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
106 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
107 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
108 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
109 |     }
110 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
111 |       if (alternate instanceof UnionType) {
112 |         UnionType union = (UnionType) alternate;
113 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
114 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
115 |         }
116 |       } else {
117 |         if (alternates.size() > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
118 |           return this;
119 |         }
120 | 
121 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
122 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
123 |         // one another.
124 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
125 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
126 |           JSType current = it.next();
127 | 
128 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
129 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
130 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
131 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
132 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
133 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
134 |               current.isNoResolvedType()) {
135 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
136 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
137 |               return this;
138 |             }
139 |           } else {
140 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
141 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
142 |               return this;
143 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
144 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
145 |               it.remove();
146 |             }
147 |           }
148 |         }
149 |         alternates.add(alternate);
150 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
151 |       }
152 |     } else {
153 |       result = null;
154 |     }
155 |     return this;
156 |   }

   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union

163 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {

===== M0197 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DeadAssignmentsElimination.isVariableStillLiveWithinExpression(Node,Node,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DeadAssignmentsElimination.java
Original range: 290-324
Documentation:
/**
   * Given a variable, node n in the tree and a sub-tree denoted by exprRoot as
   * the root, this function returns true if there exists a read of that
   * variable before a write to that variable that is on the right side of n.
   *
   * For example, suppose the node is x = 1:
   *
   * y = 1, x = 1; // false, there is no reads at all.
   * y = 1, x = 1, print(x) // true, there is a read right of n.
   * y = 1, x = 1, x = 2, print(x) // false, there is a read right of n but
   *                               // it is after a write.
   *
   * @param n The current node we should look at.
   * @param exprRoot The node
   */
Method code:
290 |   private boolean isVariableStillLiveWithinExpression(
291 |       Node n, Node exprRoot, String variable) {
292 |     while (n != exprRoot) {
293 |       VariableLiveness state = VariableLiveness.MAYBE_LIVE;
294 |       switch (n.getParent().getType()) {
295 |         case Token.OR:
296 |         case Token.AND:
297 |           // If the currently node is the first child of
298 |           // AND/OR, be conservative only consider the READs
299 |           // of the second operand.
300 | 
301 |         case Token.HOOK:
302 |           // If current node is the condition, check each following
303 |           // branch, otherwise it is a conditional branch and the
304 |           // other branch can be ignored.
305 | 
306 |         default:
307 |           for(Node sibling = n.getNext(); sibling != null;
308 |               sibling = sibling.getNext()) {
309 |             if (!ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(sibling)) {
310 |             state = isVariableReadBeforeKill(sibling, variable);
311 | 
312 |       // If we see a READ or KILL there is no need to continue.
313 |       if (state == VariableLiveness.READ) {
314 |         return true;
315 |       } else if (state == VariableLiveness.KILL) {
316 |         return false;
317 |             }
318 |           }
319 |       }
320 |       }
321 |       n = n.getParent();
322 |     }
323 |     return false;
324 |   }

===== M0198 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1396-1415
Documentation:
/**
   * Compare this node to node2 recursively and return the first pair of nodes
   * that differs doing a preorder depth-first traversal. Package private for
   * testing. Returns null if the nodes are equivalent.
   */
Method code:
1396 |   NodeMismatch checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node node2) {
1397 |     if (!isEquivalentTo(node2, false, false)) {
1398 |       return new NodeMismatch(this, node2);
1399 |     }
1400 | 
1401 |     NodeMismatch res = null;
1402 |     Node n, n2;
1403 |     for (n = first, n2 = node2.first;
1404 |          res == null && n != null;
1405 |          n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1406 |       if (node2 == null) {
1407 |         throw new IllegalStateException();
1408 |       }
1409 |       res = n.checkTreeEqualsImpl(n2);
1410 |       if (res != null) {
1411 |         return res;
1412 |       }
1413 |     }
1414 |     return res;
1415 |   }

   * Sets the prototype.
   * @param prototype the prototype. If this value is {@code null} it will
   *        silently be discarded.
   */
Method code:
286 |   public boolean setPrototype(FunctionPrototypeType prototype) {
287 |     if (prototype == null) {
288 |       return false;
289 |     }

291 |     if (isConstructor() && prototype == getInstanceType()) {

301 |         superClass.addSubType(this);
302 |       }
303 |     }
304 |

306 |       clearCachedValues();
307 |     }
308 | 
309 |     return true;
310 |   }

===== M0222 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 470-505
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
470 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
471 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
472 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
473 | 
474 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
475 | 
476 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
477 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
478 | 
479 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
480 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
481 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
482 |     }
483 | 
484 |     curNode = n;
485 |     pushScope(n);
486 | 
487 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
488 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
489 |       // scope.
490 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
491 |     }
492 | 
493 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
494 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
495 | 
496 |     // Args
497 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
498 | 
499 |     // Body
500 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
501 |             body.getType() == Token.BLOCK);
502 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
503 | 
504 |     popScope();
505 |   }

===== M0232 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFallThrough(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 779-793
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the destination node of n when we want to fallthough into the
   * subtree of n. We don't always create a CFG edge into n itself because of
   * DOs and FORs.
   */
Method code:
779 |   static Node computeFallThrough(Node n) {
780 |     switch (n.getType()) {
781 |       case Token.DO:
782 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
783 |       case Token.FOR:
784 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
785 |           return n;
786 |         }
787 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
788 |       case Token.LABEL:
789 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getLastChild());
790 |       default:
791 |         return n;
792 |     }
793 |   }

===== M0236 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleStmtList(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 482-521
Documentation:

Method code:
482 |   private void handleStmtList(Node node) {
483 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
484 |     // Special case, don't add a block of empty CATCH block to the graph.
485 |     if (node.getType() == Token.BLOCK && parent != null &&
486 |         parent.getType() == Token.TRY &&
487 |         NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node &&
488 |         !NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(node)) {
489 |       return;
490 |     }
491 | 
492 |     // A block transfer control to its first child if it is not empty.
493 |     Node child = node.getFirstChild();
494 | 
495 |     // Function declarations are skipped since control doesn't go into that
496 |     // function (unless it is called)
497 |     while (child != null && child.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
498 |       child = child.getNext();
499 |     }
500 | 
501 |     if (child != null) {
502 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFallThrough(child));
503 |     } else {
504 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFollowNode(node, this));
505 |     }
506 | 
507 |     // Synthetic blocks
508 |     if (parent != null) {
509 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
510 |         case Token.DEFAULT:
511 |         case Token.CASE:
512 |         case Token.TRY:
513 |           break;
514 |         default:
515 |           if (node.getType() == Token.BLOCK && node.isSyntheticBlock()) {
516 |             createEdge(node, Branch.SYN_BLOCK, computeFollowNode(node, this));
517 |           }
518 |           break;
519 |       }
520 |     }
521 |   }

===== M0243 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1636-1655
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the condition of an ON_TRUE / ON_FALSE CFG edge.
   * @param n a node with an outgoing conditional CFG edge
   * @return the condition node or null if the condition is not obviously a node
   */
Method code:
1636 |   static Node getConditionExpression(Node n) {
1637 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1638 |       case Token.IF:
1639 |       case Token.WHILE:
1640 |         return n.getFirstChild();
1641 |       case Token.DO:
1642 |         return n.getLastChild();
1643 |       case Token.FOR:
1644 |         switch (n.getChildCount()) {
1645 |           case 3:
1646 |             return null;
1647 |           case 4:
1648 |             return n.getFirstChild().getNext();
1649 |         }
1650 |         throw new IllegalArgumentException("malformed 'for' statement " + n);
1651 |       case Token.CASE:
1652 |         return null;
1653 |     }
1654 |     throw new IllegalArgumentException(n + " does not have a condition.");
1655 |   }

===== M0246 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LiveVariablesAnalysis.computeGenKill(Node,BitSet,BitSet,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LiveVariablesAnalysis.java
Original range: 178-263
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the GEN and KILL set.
   *
   * @param n Root node.
   * @param gen Local variables that are live because of the instruction at
   *        {@code n} will be added to this set.
   * @param kill Local variables that are killed because of the instruction at
   *        {@code n} will be added to this set.
   * @param conditional {@code true} if any assignments encountered are
   *        conditionally executed. These assignments might not kill a variable.
   */
Method code:
178 |   private void computeGenKill(Node n, BitSet gen, BitSet kill,
179 |       boolean conditional) {
180 | 
181 |     switch (n.getType()) {
182 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
183 |       case Token.BLOCK:
184 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
185 |         return;
186 | 
187 |       case Token.WHILE:
188 |       case Token.DO:
189 |       case Token.IF:
190 |         computeGenKill(NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(n), gen, kill,
191 |             conditional);
192 |         return;
193 | 
194 |       case Token.FOR:
195 |         if (!NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
196 |           computeGenKill(NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(n), gen, kill,
197 |               conditional);
198 |         } else {
199 |           // for(x in y) {...}
200 |           Node lhs = n.getFirstChild();
201 |           Node rhs = lhs.getNext();
202 |           if (NodeUtil.isVar(lhs)) {
203 |             // for(var x in y) {...}
204 |             lhs = lhs.getLastChild();
205 |           }
206 |           addToSetIfLocal(lhs, kill);
207 |           addToSetIfLocal(lhs, gen);
208 |           computeGenKill(rhs, gen, kill, conditional);
209 |         }
210 |         return;
211 | 
212 |       case Token.VAR:
213 |         for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
214 |           if (c.hasChildren()) {
215 |             computeGenKill(c.getFirstChild(), gen, kill, conditional);
216 |             if (!conditional) {
217 |               addToSetIfLocal(c, kill);
218 |             }
219 |           }
220 |         }
221 |         return;
222 | 
223 |       case Token.AND:
224 |       case Token.OR:
225 |         computeGenKill(n.getFirstChild(), gen, kill, conditional);
226 |         // May short circuit.
227 |         computeGenKill(n.getLastChild(), gen, kill, true);
228 |         return;
229 | 
230 |       case Token.HOOK:
231 |         computeGenKill(n.getFirstChild(), gen, kill, conditional);
232 |         // Assume both sides are conditional.
233 |         computeGenKill(n.getFirstChild().getNext(), gen, kill, true);
234 |         computeGenKill(n.getLastChild(), gen, kill, true);
235 |         return;
236 | 
237 |       case Token.NAME:
238 |         if (isArgumentsName(n)) {
239 |           markAllParametersEscaped();
240 |         } else {
241 |           addToSetIfLocal(n, gen);
242 |         }
243 |         return;
244 | 
245 |       default:
246 |         if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isName(n.getFirstChild())) {
247 |           Node lhs = n.getFirstChild();
248 |           if (!conditional) {
249 |             addToSetIfLocal(lhs, kill);
250 |           }
251 |           if (!NodeUtil.isAssign(n)) {
252 |             // assignments such as a += 1 reads a.
253 |             addToSetIfLocal(lhs, gen);
254 |           }
255 |           computeGenKill(lhs.getNext(), gen, kill, conditional);
256 |         } else {
257 |           for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
258 |             computeGenKill(c, gen, kill, conditional);
259 |           }
260 |         }
261 |         return;
262 |     }
263 |   }

===== M0256 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(String,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 90-132
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceName The filename.
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
90 |   public static Node parse(String sourceName,
91 |                            String sourceString,
92 |                            Config config,
93 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
94 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
95 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
96 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
97 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
98 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
99 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
100 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
101 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
102 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
103 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
104 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
105 | 
106 |     if (config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3) {
107 |       // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
108 |       compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(true);
109 |       compilerEnv.setAllowKeywordAsObjectPropertyName(true);
110 |     }
111 | 
112 |     if (config.isIdeMode) {
113 |       compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(true);
114 |     }
115 | 
116 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
117 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
118 |     try {
119 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceName, 1);
120 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
121 |       logger.info("Error parsing " + sourceName + ": " + e.getMessage());
122 |     } finally {
123 |       Context.exit();
124 |     }
125 |     Node root = null;
126 |     if (astRoot != null) {
127 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
128 |           astRoot, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
129 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
130 |     }
131 |     return root;
132 |   }

===== M0257 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.checkFirstModule(List<JSModule>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 375-383
Documentation:
/**
   * Verifies that at least one module has been provided and that the first one
   * has at least one source code input.
   */
Method code:
375 |   private void checkFirstModule(List<JSModule> modules) {
376 |     if (modules.isEmpty()) {
377 |       report(JSError.make(EMPTY_MODULE_LIST_ERROR));
378 |     } else if (modules.get(0).getInputs().isEmpty() && modules.size() > 1) {
379 |       // The root module may only be empty if there is exactly 1 module.
380 |       report(JSError.make(EMPTY_ROOT_MODULE_ERROR,
381 |           modules.get(0).getName()));
382 |     }
383 |   }

===== M0258 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.getAllInputsFromModules(List<JSModule>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 411-427
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
411 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
412 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
413 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
414 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
415 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
416 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {
417 |         String inputName = input.getName();
418 | 
419 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): If an input is in more than one module,
420 |         // it will show up twice in the inputs list, and then we
421 |         // will get an error down the line.
422 |         inputs.add(input);
423 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
424 |       }
425 |     }
426 |     return inputs;
427 |   }

1875 |   public Node cloneTree() {
1876 |     Node result = cloneNode();
1877 |     for (Node n2 = getFirstChild(); n2 != null; n2 = n2.getNext()) {

1879 |       n2clone.parent = result;

1887 |     }
1888 |     return result;
1889 |   }

===== M0277 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 400-415
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
400 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
401 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
402 |       Set<String> directives = null;
403 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
404 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
405 |         if (directives == null) {
406 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
407 |         } else {
408 |           directives.add(directive);
409 |         }
410 |       }
411 | 
412 |       if (directives != null) {
413 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
414 |       }
415 |     }

===== M0278 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.cloneNode()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1856-1870
Documentation:
/**
   * @return A detached clone of the Node, specifically excluding its children.
   */
Method code:
1856 |   public Node cloneNode() {
1857 |     Node result;
1858 |     try {
1859 |       result = (Node) super.clone();
1860 |       // PropListItem lists are immutable and can be shared so there is no
1861 |       // need to clone them here.
1862 |       result.next = null;
1863 |       result.first = null;
1864 |       result.last = null;
1865 |       result.parent = null;
1866 |     } catch (CloneNotSupportedException e) {

===== M0279 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1583-1642
Documentation:
/**
   * @param compareJsType Whether to compare the JSTypes of the nodes.
   * @param recurse Whether to compare the children of the current node, if
   *    not only the the count of the children are compared.
   * @return Whether this node is equivalent semantically to the provided node.
   */
Method code:
1583 |   boolean isEquivalentTo(Node node, boolean compareJsType, boolean recurse) {
1584 |     if (type != node.getType()
1585 |         || getChildCount() != node.getChildCount()
1586 |         || getNodeClass(this) != getNodeClass(node)) {
1587 |       return false;
1588 |     }
1589 | 
1590 |     if (compareJsType && !JSType.isEquivalent(jsType, node.getJSType())) {
1591 |       return false;
1592 |     }
1593 | 
1594 |     if (type == Token.ARRAYLIT) {
1595 |       try {
1596 |         int[] indices1 = (int[]) getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1597 |         int[] indices2 = (int[]) node.getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1598 |         if (indices1 == null) {
1599 |           if (indices2 != null) {
1600 |             return false;
1601 |           }
1602 |         } else if (indices2 == null) {
1603 |           return false;
1604 |         } else if (indices1.length != indices2.length) {
1605 |           return false;
1606 |         } else {
1607 |           for (int i = 0; i < indices1.length; i++) {
1608 |             if (indices1[i] != indices2[i]) {
1609 |               return false;
1610 |             }
1611 |           }
1612 |         }
1613 |       } catch (Exception e) {
1614 |         return false;
1615 |       }
1616 |     } else if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1617 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1618 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1619 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1620 |         return false;
1621 |       }
1622 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING) {
1623 |       int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1624 |       int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1625 |       if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1626 |         return false;
1627 |       }
1628 |     }
1629 | 
1630 |     if (recurse) {
1631 |       Node n, n2;
1632 |       for (n = first, n2 = node.first;
1633 |            n != null;
1634 |            n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1635 |         if (!n.isEquivalentTo(n2, compareJsType, true)) {
1636 |           return false;
1637 |         }
1638 |       }
1639 |     }
1640 | 
1641 |     return true;
1642 |   }

459 |     @Override
460 |     void maybeCutLine() {
461 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold) {
462 |         // Use the preferred position provided it will break the line.
463 |         if (preferredBreakPosition > lineStartPosition &&
464 |             preferredBreakPosition < lineStartPosition + lineLength) {
465 |           int position = preferredBreakPosition;

893 |   public static boolean mayThrowException(Node n) {
894 |     switch (n.getType()) {
895 |       case Token.CALL:
896 |       case Token.GETPROP:

898 |       case Token.THROW:

===== M0320 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.Tracer(String,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 234-277
Documentation:
/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totalling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
234 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
235 |     this.type = type;
236 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
237 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
238 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
239 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
240 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
241 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
242 |       int i = 0;
243 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
244 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
245 |         i++;
246 |       }
247 |     }
248 | 
249 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
250 | 
251 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
252 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
253 |       return;
254 |     }
255 | 
256 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
257 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {

259 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
260 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
261 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
262 |       trace.truncateEvents();
263 |     }
264 | 
265 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
266 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
267 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
268 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
269 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
270 |                     + "try/finally block. "
271 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
272 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
273 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
274 |     }
275 | 
276 |     trace.startEvent(this);
277 |   }

===== M0323 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.analyze(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 201-229
Documentation:
/**
   * Finds a fixed-point solution. The function has the side effect of replacing
   * the existing node annotations with the computed solutions using {@link
   * com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.GraphNode#setAnnotation(Annotation)}.
   *
   * <p>Initially, each node's input and output flow state contains the value
   * given by {@link #createInitialEstimateLattice()} (with the exception of the
   * entry node of the graph which takes on the {@link #createEntryLattice()}
   * value. Each node will use the output state of its predecessor and compute a
   * output state according to the instruction. At that time, any nodes that
   * depends on the node's newly modified output value will need to recompute
   * their output state again. Each step will perform a computation at one node
   * until no extra computation will modify any existing output state anymore.
   *
   * @param maxSteps Max number of iterations before the method stops and throw
   *        a {@link MaxIterationsExceededException}. This will prevent the
   *        analysis from going into a infinite loop.
   */
Method code:
201 |   final void analyze(int maxSteps) {
202 |     initialize();
203 |     int step = 0;
204 |     while (!orderedWorkSet.isEmpty()) {
205 |       if (step > maxSteps) {
206 |         throw new MaxIterationsExceededException(
207 |           "Analysis did not terminate after " + maxSteps + " iterations");
208 |       }
209 |       DiGraphNode<N, Branch> curNode = orderedWorkSet.iterator().next();
210 |       orderedWorkSet.remove(curNode);
211 |       joinInputs(curNode);
212 |       if (flow(curNode)) {
213 |         // If there is a change in the current node, we want to grab the list
214 |         // of nodes that this node affects.
215 |         List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> nextNodes = isForward() ?
216 |             cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(curNode) :
217 |             cfg.getDirectedPredNodes(curNode);
218 |         for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> nextNode : nextNodes) {
219 |           if (nextNode != cfg.getImplicitReturn()) {
220 |             orderedWorkSet.add(nextNode);
221 |           }
222 |         }
223 |       }
224 |       step++;
225 |     }
226 |     if (isForward()) {
227 |       joinInputs(getCfg().getImplicitReturn());
228 |     }
229 |   }

===== M0329 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 541-598
Documentation:

Method code:
541 |     @Override
542 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
543 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
544 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
545 |       if (name == null) {
546 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
547 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
548 |           errorReporter.error(
549 |             "unnamed function statement",
550 |             sourceName,
551 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
552 |         }
553 |         name = new Name();
554 |         name.setIdentifier("");
555 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
556 |       }
557 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
558 |       Node newName = transform(name);
559 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
560 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
561 |         // declaration.
562 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
563 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
564 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
565 |         // same line as the function keyword.
566 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
567 |             functionNode.getLp();
568 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
569 |       }
570 | 
571 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
572 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.LP);
573 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
574 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
575 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
576 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
577 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
578 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
579 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
580 |       if (fnName != null) {
581 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
582 |       } else {
583 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
584 |       }
585 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
586 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
587 | 
588 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
589 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
590 |         lp.addChildToBack(transform(param));
591 |       }
592 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
593 | 
594 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
595 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
596 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
597 |      return node;
598 |     }

===== M0348 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 217-292
Documentation:

Method code:
217 |   @Override
218 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
219 |       NodeTraversal nodeTraversal, Node n, Node parent) {
220 |     astPosition.put(n, astPositionCounter++);
221 | 
222 |     switch (n.getType()) {
223 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
224 |         if (shouldTraverseFunctions || n == cfg.getEntry().getValue()) {
225 |           exceptionHandler.push(n);
226 |           return true;
227 |         }
228 |         return false;
229 |       case Token.TRY:
230 |         exceptionHandler.push(n);
231 |         return true;
232 |     }
233 | 
234 |     /*
235 |      * We are going to stop the traversal depending on what the node's parent
236 |      * is.
237 |      *
238 |      * We are only interested in adding edges between nodes that change control
239 |      * flow. The most obvious ones are loops and IF-ELSE's. A statement
240 |      * transfers control to its next sibling.
241 |      *
242 |      * In case of an expression tree, there is no control flow within the tree
243 |      * even when there are short circuited operators and conditionals. When we
244 |      * are doing data flow analysis, we will simply synthesize lattices up the
245 |      * expression tree by finding the meet at each expression node.
246 |      *
247 |      * For example: within a Token.SWITCH, the expression in question does not
248 |      * change the control flow and need not to be considered.
249 |      */
250 |     if (parent != null) {
251 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
252 |         case Token.FOR:
253 |           // Only traverse the body of the for loop.
254 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
255 | 
256 |         // Skip the conditions.
257 |         case Token.IF:
258 |         case Token.WHILE:
259 |         case Token.WITH:
260 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
261 |         case Token.DO:
262 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
263 |         // Only traverse the body of the cases
264 |         case Token.SWITCH:
265 |         case Token.CASE:
266 |         case Token.CATCH:
267 |         case Token.LABEL:
268 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
269 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
270 |           return n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
271 |         case Token.CONTINUE:
272 |         case Token.BREAK:
273 |         case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
274 |         case Token.VAR:
275 |         case Token.RETURN:
276 |         case Token.THROW:
277 |           return false;
278 |         case Token.TRY:
279 |           /* Just before we are about to visit the second child of the TRY node,
280 |            * we know that we will be visiting either the CATCH or the FINALLY.
281 |            * In other words, we know that the post order traversal of the TRY
282 |            * block has been finished, no more exceptions can be caught by the
283 |            * handler at this TRY block and should be taken out of the stack.
284 |            */
285 |           if (n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext()) {
286 |             Preconditions.checkState(exceptionHandler.peek() == parent);
287 |             exceptionHandler.pop();
288 |           }
289 |       }
290 |     }
291 |     return true;
292 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.tokenToName(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1441-1562
Documentation:

Method code:
1441 |   public static String tokenToName(int token) {
1442 |     switch (token) {
1443 |       case Token.ERROR:           return "error";
1444 |       case Token.EOF:             return "eof";
1445 |       case Token.EOL:             return "eol";
1446 |       case Token.ENTERWITH:       return "enterwith";
1447 |       case Token.LEAVEWITH:       return "leavewith";
1448 |       case Token.RETURN:          return "return";
1449 |       case Token.GOTO:            return "goto";
1450 |       case Token.IFEQ:            return "ifeq";
1451 |       case Token.IFNE:            return "ifne";
1452 |       case Token.SETNAME:         return "setname";
1453 |       case Token.BITOR:           return "bitor";
1454 |       case Token.BITXOR:          return "bitxor";

===== M0364 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.has(Node,Predicate<Node>,Predicate<Node>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java

/**
   * @return Whether the predicate is true for the node or any of its children.
   */
Method code:
2628 |   static boolean has(Node node,
2629 |                      Predicate<Node> pred,
2630 |                      Predicate<Node> traverseChildrenPred) {
2631 |     if (pred.apply(node)) {
2632 |       return true;
2633 |     }
2634 | 
2635 |     if (!traverseChildrenPred.apply(node)) {
2636 |       return false;
2637 |     }
2638 | 
2639 |     for (Node c = node.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
2640 |       if (has(c, pred, traverseChildrenPred)) {
2641 |         return true;
2642 |       }
2643 |     }
2644 | 
2645 |     return false;
2646 |   }

===== M0377 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFollowNode(Node,Node,ControlFlowAnalysis)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 675-772
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the follow() node of a given node and its parent. There is a side
   * effect when calling this function. If this function computed an edge that
   * exists a FINALLY, it'll attempt to connect the fromNode to the outer
   * FINALLY according to the finallyMap.
   *
   * @param fromNode The original source node since {@code node} is changed
   *        during recursion.
   * @param node The node that follow() should compute.
   */
Method code:
675 |   private static Node computeFollowNode(
676 |       Node fromNode, Node node, ControlFlowAnalysis cfa) {
677 |     /*
678 |      * This is the case where:
679 |      *
680 |      * 1. Parent is null implies that we are transferring control to the end of
681 |      * the script.
682 |      *
683 |      * 2. Parent is a function implies that we are transferring control back to
684 |      * the caller of the function.
685 |      *
686 |      * 3. If the node is a return statement, we should also transfer control
687 |      * back to the caller of the function.
688 |      *
689 |      * 4. If the node is root then we have reached the end of what we have been
690 |      * asked to traverse.
691 |      *
692 |      * In all cases we should transfer control to a "symbolic return" node.
693 |      * This will make life easier for DFAs.
694 |      */
695 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
696 |     if (parent == null || parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION ||
697 |         (cfa != null && node == cfa.root)) {
698 |       return null;
699 |     }
700 | 
701 |     // If we are just before a IF/WHILE/DO/FOR:
702 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
703 |       // The follow() of any of the path from IF would be what follows IF.
704 |       case Token.IF:
705 |         return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
706 |       case Token.CASE:
707 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
708 |         // After the body of a CASE, the control goes to the body of the next
709 |         // case, without having to go to the case condition.
710 |         if (parent.getNext() != null) {
711 |           if (parent.getNext().getType() == Token.CASE) {
712 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild().getNext();
713 |           } else if (parent.getNext().getType() == Token.DEFAULT) {
714 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild();
715 |           } else {
716 |             Preconditions.checkState(false, "Not reachable");
717 |           }
718 |         } else {
719 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
720 |         }
721 |         break;
722 |       case Token.FOR:
723 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
724 |           return parent;
725 |         } else {
726 |           return parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
727 |         }
728 |       case Token.WHILE:
729 |       case Token.DO:
730 |         return parent;
731 |       case Token.TRY:
732 |         // If we are coming out of the TRY block...
733 |         if (parent.getFirstChild() == node) {
734 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
735 |             return computeFallThrough(parent.getLastChild());
736 |           } else { // and have no FINALLY.
737 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
738 |           }
739 |         // CATCH block.
740 |         } else if (NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node){
741 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
742 |             return computeFallThrough(node.getNext());
743 |           } else {
744 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
745 |           }
746 |         // If we are coming out of the FINALLY block...
747 |         } else if (parent.getLastChild() == node){
748 |           if (cfa != null) {
749 |             for (Node finallyNode : cfa.finallyMap.get(parent)) {
750 |               cfa.createEdge(fromNode, Branch.UNCOND, finallyNode);
751 |             }
752 |           }
753 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
754 |         }
755 |     }
756 | 
757 |     // Now that we are done with the special cases follow should be its
758 |     // immediate sibling, unless its sibling is a function
759 |     Node nextSibling = node.getNext();
760 | 
761 |     // Skip function declarations because control doesn't get pass into it.
762 |     while (nextSibling != null && nextSibling.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
763 |       nextSibling = nextSibling.getNext();
764 |     }
765 | 
766 |     if (nextSibling != null) {
767 |       return computeFallThrough(nextSibling);
768 |     } else {
769 |       // If there are no more siblings, control is transfered up the AST.
770 |       return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
771 |     }
772 |   }

===== M0378 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 148-190
Documentation:

Method code:
148 |   @Override
149 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
150 |     this.root = root;
151 |     astPositionCounter = 0;
152 |     astPosition = Maps.newHashMap();
153 |     nodePriorities = Maps.newHashMap();
154 |     cfg = new AstControlFlowGraph(computeFallThrough(root), nodePriorities,
155 |                                   edgeAnnotations);
156 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, this);
157 |     astPosition.put(null, ++astPositionCounter); // the implicit return is last.
158 | 
159 |     // Now, generate the priority of nodes by doing a depth-first
160 |     // search on the CFG.
161 |     priorityCounter = 0;
162 |     DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry = cfg.getEntry();
163 |     prioritizeFromEntryNode(entry);
164 | 
165 |     if (shouldTraverseFunctions) {
166 |       // If we're traversing inner functions, we need to rank the
167 |       // priority of them too.
168 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
169 |         Node value = candidate.getValue();
170 |         if (value != null && value.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
171 |           Preconditions.checkState(
172 |               !nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate) || candidate == entry);
173 |           prioritizeFromEntryNode(candidate);
174 |         }
175 |       }
176 |     }
177 | 
178 |     // At this point, all reachable nodes have been given a priority, but
179 |     // unreachable nodes have not been given a priority. Put them last.
180 |     // Presumably, it doesn't really matter what priority they get, since
181 |     // this shouldn't happen in real code.
182 |     for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
183 |       if (!nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate)) {
184 |         nodePriorities.put(candidate, ++priorityCounter);
185 |       }
186 |     }
187 | 
188 |     // Again, the implicit return node is always last.
189 |     nodePriorities.put(cfg.getImplicitReturn(), ++priorityCounter);
190 |   }

   * is not defined.
   */
