===== M0391 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1902-1904
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1902 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1903 |     return n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION && !isStatement(n);
1904 |   }

===== M0399 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LiveVariablesAnalysis.flowThrough(Node,LiveVariableLattice)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LiveVariablesAnalysis.java
Original range: 146-165
Documentation:

Method code:
146 |   @Override
147 |   LiveVariableLattice flowThrough(Node node, LiveVariableLattice input) {
148 |     final BitSet gen = new BitSet(input.liveSet.size());
149 |     final BitSet kill = new BitSet(input.liveSet.size());
150 | 
151 |     // Make kills conditional if the node can end abruptly by an exception.
152 |     boolean conditional = false;
153 |     List<DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch>> edgeList = getCfg().getOutEdges(node);
154 |     for (DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch> edge : edgeList) {
155 |       if (Branch.ON_EX.equals(edge.getValue())) {
156 |         conditional = true;
157 |       }
158 |     }
159 |     computeGenKill(node, gen, kill, conditional);
160 |     LiveVariableLattice result = new LiveVariableLattice(input);
161 |     // L_in = L_out - Kill + Gen
162 |     result.liveSet.andNot(kill);
163 |     result.liveSet.or(gen);
164 |     return result;
165 |   }

===== M0409 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DeadAssignmentsElimination.tryRemoveAssignment(NodeTraversal,Node,Node,FlowState<LiveVariableLattice>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DeadAssignmentsElimination.java
Original range: 169-273
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines if any local variables are dead after the instruction {@code n}
   * and are assigned within the subtree of {@code n}. Removes those assignments
   * if there are any.
   *
   * @param n Target instruction.
   * @param exprRoot The CFG node where the liveness information in state is
   *     still correct.
   * @param state The liveness information at {@code n}.
   */
Method code:
169 |   private void tryRemoveAssignment(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node exprRoot,
170 |       FlowState<LiveVariableLattice> state) {
171 | 
172 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
173 | 
174 |     if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) ||
175 |         n.getType() == Token.INC || n.getType() == Token.DEC) {
176 | 
177 |       Node lhs = n.getFirstChild();
178 |       Node rhs = lhs.getNext();
179 | 
180 |       // Recurse first. Example: dead_x = dead_y = 1; We try to clean up dead_y
181 |       // first.
182 |       if (rhs != null) {
183 |         tryRemoveAssignment(t, rhs, exprRoot, state);
184 |         rhs = lhs.getNext();
185 |       }
186 | 
187 |       Scope scope = t.getScope();
188 |       if (!NodeUtil.isName(lhs)) {
189 |         return; // Not a local variable assignment.
190 |       }
191 |       String name = lhs.getString();
192 |       if (!scope.isDeclared(name, false)) {
193 |         return;
194 |       }
195 |       Var var = scope.getVar(name);
196 | 
197 |       if (liveness.getEscapedLocals().contains(var)) {
198 |         return; // Local variable that might be escaped due to closures.
199 |       }
200 | 
201 |       // If we have an identity assignment such as a=a, always remove it
202 |       // regardless of what the liveness results because it
203 |       // does not change the result afterward.
204 |       if (rhs != null &&
205 |           NodeUtil.isName(rhs) &&
206 |           rhs.getString().equals(var.name) &&
207 |           NodeUtil.isAssign(n)) {
208 |         n.removeChild(rhs);
209 |         n.getParent().replaceChild(n, rhs);
210 |         compiler.reportCodeChange();
211 |         return;
212 |       }
213 | 
214 |       if (state.getOut().isLive(var)) {
215 |         return; // Variable not dead.
216 |       }
217 | 
218 |       if (state.getIn().isLive(var) &&
219 |           isVariableStillLiveWithinExpression(n, exprRoot, var.name)) {
220 |         // The variable is killed here but it is also live before it.
221 |         // This is possible if we have say:
222 |         //    if (X = a && a = C) {..} ; .......; a = S;
223 |         // In this case we are safe to remove "a = C" because it is dead.
224 |         // However if we have:
225 |         //    if (a = C && X = a) {..} ; .......; a = S;
226 |         // removing "a = C" is NOT correct, although the live set at the node
227 |         // is exactly the same.
228 |         // TODO(user): We need more fine grain CFA or we need to keep track
229 |         // of GEN sets when we recurse here.
230 |         return;
231 |       }
232 | 
233 |       if (NodeUtil.isAssign(n)) {
234 |         n.removeChild(rhs);
235 |         n.getParent().replaceChild(n, rhs);
236 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n)) {
237 |         n.removeChild(rhs);
238 |         n.removeChild(lhs);
239 |         Node op = new Node(NodeUtil.getOpFromAssignmentOp(n), lhs, rhs);
240 |         parent.replaceChild(n, op);
241 |       } else if (n.getType() == Token.INC || n.getType() == Token.DEC) {
242 |         if (NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(parent)) {
243 |           parent.replaceChild(n,
244 |               new Node(Token.VOID, Node.newNumber(0).copyInformationFrom(n)));
245 |         } else if(n.getType() == Token.COMMA && n != parent.getLastChild()) {
246 |           parent.removeChild(n);
247 |         } else if (parent.getType() == Token.FOR && !NodeUtil.isForIn(parent) &&
248 |             NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n) {
249 |           parent.replaceChild(n, new Node(Token.EMPTY));
250 |         } else {
251 |           // Cannot replace x = a++ with x = a because that's not valid
252 |           // when a is not a number.
253 |           return;
254 |         }
255 |       } else {
256 |         // Not reachable.
257 |         Preconditions.checkState(false, "Unknown statement");
258 |       }
259 | 
260 |       compiler.reportCodeChange();
261 |       return;
262 | 
263 |     } else {
264 |       for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null;) {
265 |         Node next = c.getNext();
266 |         if (!ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(c)) {
267 |           tryRemoveAssignment(t, c, exprRoot, state);
268 |         }
269 |         c = next;
270 |       }
271 |       return;
272 |     }
273 |   }

===== M0410 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.computeEscaped(Scope,Set<Var>,AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 548-578
Documentation:
/**
   * Compute set of escaped variables. When a variable is escaped in a
   * dataflow analysis, it can be reference outside of the code that we are
   * analyzing. A variable is escaped if any of the following is true:
   *
   * <p><ol>
   * <li>It is defined as the exception name in CATCH clause so it became a
   * variable local not to our definition of scope.</li>
   * <li>Exported variables as they can be needed after the script terminates.
   * </li>
   * <li>Names of named functions because in javascript, <i>function foo(){}</i>
   * does not kill <i>foo</i> in the dataflow.</li>
   */
Method code:
548 |   static void computeEscaped(final Scope jsScope, final Set<Var> escaped,
549 |       AbstractCompiler compiler) {
550 |     // TODO(user): Very good place to store this information somewhere.
551 |     AbstractPostOrderCallback finder = new AbstractPostOrderCallback() {
552 |       @Override
553 |       public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
554 |         if (jsScope == t.getScope() || !NodeUtil.isName(n)
555 |             || NodeUtil.isFunction(parent)) {
556 |           return;
557 |         }
558 |         String name = n.getString();
559 |         Var var = t.getScope().getVar(name);
560 |         if (var != null && var.scope == jsScope) {
561 |           escaped.add(jsScope.getVar(name));
562 |         }
563 |       }
564 |     };
565 | 
566 |     NodeTraversal t = new NodeTraversal(compiler, finder);
567 |     t.traverseAtScope(jsScope);
568 | 
569 |     // 1: Remove the exception name in CATCH which technically isn't local to
570 |     //    begin with.
571 |     for (Iterator<Var> i = jsScope.getVars(); i.hasNext();) {
572 |       Var var = i.next();
573 |       if (var.getParentNode().getType() == Token.CATCH ||
574 |           compiler.getCodingConvention().isExported(var.getName())) {
575 |         escaped.add(var);
576 |       }
577 |     }
578 |   }

===== M0413 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 55-73
Documentation:

Method code:
55 |   @Override
56 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
57 |     if (checkOnly) {
58 |       normalizeNodeTypes(root);
59 |     } else {
60 |       // Don't perform "PrepareAnnotations" when doing checks as
61 |       // they currently aren't valid during sanity checks.  In particular,
62 |       // they DIRECT_EVAL shouldn't be applied after inlining has been
63 |       // performed.
64 |       if (externs != null) {
65 |         NodeTraversal.traverse(
66 |             compiler, externs, new PrepareAnnotations(compiler));
67 |       }
68 |       if (root != null) {
69 |         NodeTraversal.traverse(

===== M0433 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.joinInputs(DiGraphNode<N, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 286-324
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the new flow state at a given node's entry by merging the
   * output (input) lattice of the node's predecessor (successor).
   *
   * @param node Node to compute new join.
   */
Method code:
286 |   protected void joinInputs(DiGraphNode<N, Branch> node) {
287 |     FlowState<L> state = node.getAnnotation();
288 |     if (isForward()) {
289 |       if (cfg.getEntry() == node) {
290 |         state.setIn(createEntryLattice());
291 |       } else {
292 |         List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> inNodes = cfg.getDirectedPredNodes(node);
293 |         if (inNodes.size() == 1) {
294 |           FlowState<L> inNodeState = inNodes.get(0).getAnnotation();
295 |           state.setIn(inNodeState.getOut());
296 |         } else if (inNodes.size() > 1) {
297 |           List<L> values = new ArrayList<L>(inNodes.size());
298 |           for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> currentNode : inNodes) {
299 |             FlowState<L> currentNodeState = currentNode.getAnnotation();
300 |             values.add(currentNodeState.getOut());
301 |           }
302 |           state.setIn(joinOp.apply(values));
303 |         }
304 |       }
305 |     } else {
306 |       List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> inNodes = cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(node);
307 |       if (inNodes.size() == 1) {
308 |         DiGraphNode<N, Branch> inNode = inNodes.get(0);
309 |         if (inNode == cfg.getImplicitReturn()) {
310 |           state.setOut(createEntryLattice());
311 |         } else {
312 |           FlowState<L> inNodeState = inNode.getAnnotation();
313 |           state.setOut(inNodeState.getIn());
314 |         }
315 |       } else if (inNodes.size() > 1) {
316 |         List<L> values = new ArrayList<L>(inNodes.size());
317 |         for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> currentNode : inNodes) {
318 |           FlowState<L> currentNodeState = currentNode.getAnnotation();
319 |           values.add(currentNodeState.getIn());
320 |         }
321 |         state.setOut(joinOp.apply(values));
322 |       }
323 |     }
324 |   }

===== M0436 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DeadAssignmentsElimination.isVariableReadBeforeKill(Node,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DeadAssignmentsElimination.java
Original range: 338-382
Documentation:
/**
   * Give an expression and a variable. It returns READ, if the first
   * reference of that variable is a read. It returns KILL, if the first
   * reference of that variable is an assignment. It returns MAY_LIVE otherwise.
   */
Method code:
338 |   private VariableLiveness isVariableReadBeforeKill(
339 |       Node n, String variable) {
340 | 
341 |     if (NodeUtil.isName(n) && variable.equals(n.getString())) {
342 |       if (NodeUtil.isLhs(n, n.getParent())) {
343 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getParent().getType() == Token.ASSIGN);
344 |         // The expression to which the assignment is made is evaluated before
345 |         // the RHS is evaluated (normal left to right evaluation) but the KILL
346 |         // occurs after the RHS is evaluated.
347 |         Node rhs = n.getNext();
348 |         VariableLiveness state = isVariableReadBeforeKill(rhs, variable);
349 |         if (state == VariableLiveness.READ) {
350 |           return state;
351 |         }
352 |         return VariableLiveness.KILL;
353 |       } else {
354 |         return VariableLiveness.READ;
355 |       }
356 |     }
357 | 
358 |     switch (n.getType()) {
359 |       // Conditionals
360 |       case Token.OR:
361 |       case Token.AND:
362 |         // With a AND/OR the first branch always runs, but the second is
363 |         // may not.
364 |       case Token.HOOK:
365 |         return checkHookBranchReadBeforeKill(
366 |             n.getFirstChild().getNext(), n.getLastChild(), variable);
367 | 
368 |       default:
369 |         // Expressions are evaluated left-right, depth first.
370 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
371 |             child != null; child = child.getNext()) {
372 |           if (!ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(child)) { // Not a FUNCTION
373 |           VariableLiveness state = isVariableReadBeforeKill(child, variable);
374 |           if (state != VariableLiveness.MAYBE_LIVE) {
375 |             return state;
376 |           }
377 |         }
378 |       }
379 |     }
380 | 
381 |     return VariableLiveness.MAYBE_LIVE;
382 |   }

===== M0440 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DeadAssignmentsElimination.enterScope(NodeTraversal)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DeadAssignmentsElimination.java
Original range: 69-99
Documentation:

Method code:
69 |   @Override
70 |   public void enterScope(NodeTraversal t) {
71 |     Scope scope = t.getScope();
72 |     // Global scope _SHOULD_ work, however, liveness won't finish without
73 |     // -Xmx1024 in closure. We might have to look at coding conventions for
74 |     // exported variables as well.
75 |     if (scope.isGlobal()) {
76 |       return;
77 |     }
78 | 
79 |     // We are not going to do any dead assignment elimination in when there is
80 |     // at least one inner function because in most browsers, when there is a
81 |     // closure, ALL the variables are saved (escaped).
82 |     Node fnBlock = t.getScopeRoot().getLastChild();
83 |     if (NodeUtil.containsFunction(fnBlock)) {
84 |       return;
85 |     }
86 | 
87 |     // We don't do any dead assignment elimination if there are no assigns
88 |     // to eliminate. :)
89 |     if (!NodeUtil.has(fnBlock, matchRemovableAssigns,
90 |             Predicates.<Node>alwaysTrue())) {
91 |       return;
92 |     }
93 | 
94 |     // Computes liveness information first.
95 |     ControlFlowGraph<Node> cfg = t.getControlFlowGraph();
96 |     liveness = new LiveVariablesAnalysis(cfg, scope, compiler);
97 |     liveness.analyze();
98 |     tryRemoveDeadAssignments(t, cfg);
99 |   }

===== M0444 =====

72 |   void addGuard(WarningsGuard guard) {
73 |     if (guard instanceof ComposeWarningsGuard) {
74 |       // Reverse the guards, so that they have the same order in the result.
75 |       addGuards(((ComposeWarningsGuard) guard).guards.descendingSet());
76 |     } else {
77 |       numberOfAdds++;
78 |       orderOfAddition.put(guard, numberOfAdds);
79 |       guards.remove(guard);
80 |       guards.add(guard);
81 |     }
82 |   }

===== M0447 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.add(Node,Context)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 82-720
Documentation:

Method code:
82 |   void add(Node n, Context context) {
83 |     if (!cc.continueProcessing()) {
84 |       return;
85 |     }
86 | 
87 |     int type = n.getType();
88 |     String opstr = NodeUtil.opToStr(type);
89 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
90 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();
91 |     Node last = n.getLastChild();
92 | 
93 |     // Handle all binary operators
94 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
95 |       Preconditions.checkState(
96 |           childCount == 2,
97 |           "Bad binary operator \"%s\": expected 2 arguments but got %s",
98 |           opstr, childCount);
99 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
100 |       addLeftExpr(first, p, context);
101 |       cc.addOp(opstr, true);
102 | 
103 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
104 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
105 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
106 | 
107 |       // Handle associativity.
108 |       // e.g. if the parse tree is a * (b * c),
109 |       // we can simply generate a * b * c.
110 |       if (last.getType() == type &&
111 |           NodeUtil.isAssociative(type)) {
112 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
113 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
114 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
115 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
116 |       } else {
117 |         addExpr(last, p + 1, rhsContext);
118 |       }
119 |       return;
120 |     }
121 | 
122 |     cc.startSourceMapping(n);
123 | 
124 |     switch (type) {
125 |       case Token.TRY: {
126 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getNext().getType() == Token.BLOCK &&
127 |                 !first.getNext().hasMoreThanOneChild());
128 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount >= 2 && childCount <= 3);
129 | 
130 |         add("try");
131 |         add(first, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
132 | 
133 |         // second child contains the catch block, or nothing if there
134 |         // isn't a catch block
135 |         Node catchblock = first.getNext().getFirstChild();
136 |         if (catchblock != null) {
137 |           add(catchblock);
138 |         }
139 | 
140 |         if (childCount == 3) {
141 |           add("finally");
142 |           add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
143 |         }
144 |         break;
145 |       }
146 | 
147 |       case Token.CATCH:
148 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
149 |         add("catch(");
150 |         add(first);
151 |         add(")");
152 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
153 |         break;
154 | 
155 |       case Token.THROW:
156 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
157 |         add("throw");
158 |         add(first);
159 | 
160 |         // Must have a ';' after a throw statement, otherwise safari can't
161 |         // parse this.
162 |         cc.endStatement(true);
163 |         break;
164 | 
165 |       case Token.RETURN:
166 |         add("return");
167 |         if (childCount == 1) {
168 |           add(first);
169 |         } else {
170 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
171 |         }
172 |         cc.endStatement();
173 |         break;
174 | 
175 |       case Token.VAR:
176 |         if (first != null) {
177 |           add("var ");
178 |           addList(first, false, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
179 |         }
180 |         break;
181 | 
182 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
183 |         Preconditions.checkState(!n.getString().isEmpty());
184 |         addIdentifier(n.getString());
185 |         break;
186 | 
187 |       case Token.NAME:
188 |         if (first == null || first.getType() == Token.EMPTY) {
189 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
190 |         } else {
191 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
192 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
193 |           cc.addOp("=", true);
194 |           if (first.getType() == Token.COMMA) {
195 |             addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.ASSIGN));
196 |           } else {
197 |             // Add expression, consider nearby code at lowest level of
198 |             // precedence.
199 |             addExpr(first, 0, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
200 |           }
201 |         }
202 |         break;
203 | 
204 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
205 |         add("[");
206 |         addArrayList(first);
207 |         add("]");
208 |         break;
209 | 
210 |       case Token.LP:
211 |         add("(");
212 |         addList(first);
213 |         add(")");
214 |         break;
215 | 
216 |       case Token.COMMA:
217 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
218 |         addList(first, false, context);
219 |         break;
220 | 
221 |       case Token.NUMBER:
222 |         Preconditions.checkState(
223 |             childCount ==
224 |             ((n.getParent() != null &&
225 |               n.getParent().getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) ? 1 : 0));
226 |         cc.addNumber(n.getDouble());
227 |         break;
228 | 
229 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
230 |       case Token.VOID:
231 |       case Token.NOT:
232 |       case Token.BITNOT:
233 |       case Token.POS: {
234 |         // All of these unary operators are right-associative
235 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
236 |         cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
237 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type));
238 |         break;
239 |       }
240 | 
241 |       case Token.NEG: {
242 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
243 | 
244 |         // It's important to our sanity checker that the code
245 |         // we print produces the same AST as the code we parse back.
246 |         // NEG is a weird case because Rhino parses "- -2" as "2".
247 |         if (n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NUMBER) {
248 |           cc.addNumber(-n.getFirstChild().getDouble());
249 |         } else {
250 |           cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
251 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type));
252 |         }
253 | 
254 |         break;
255 |       }
256 | 
257 |       case Token.HOOK: {
258 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
259 |         int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
260 |         addLeftExpr(first, p + 1, context);
261 |         cc.addOp("?", true);
262 |         addExpr(first.getNext(), 1);
263 |         cc.addOp(":", true);
264 |         addExpr(last, 1);
265 |         break;
266 |       }
267 | 
268 |       case Token.REGEXP:
269 |         if (first.getType() != Token.STRING ||
270 |             last.getType() != Token.STRING) {
271 |           throw new Error("Expected children to be strings");
272 |         }
273 | 
274 |         String regexp = regexpEscape(first.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder);
275 | 
276 |         // I only use one .add because whitespace matters
277 |         if (childCount == 2) {
278 |           add(regexp + last.getString());
279 |         } else {
280 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
281 |           add(regexp);
282 |         }
283 |         break;
284 | 
285 |       case Token.GET_REF:
286 |         add(first);
287 |         break;
288 | 
289 |       case Token.REF_SPECIAL:
290 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
291 |         add(first);
292 |         add(".");
293 |         add((String) n.getProp(Node.NAME_PROP));
294 |         break;
295 | 
296 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
297 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
298 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
299 |         }
300 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
301 |         boolean funcNeedsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
302 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
303 |           add("(");
304 |         }
305 | 
306 |         add("function");
307 |         add(first);
308 | 
309 |         add(first.getNext());
310 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
311 |         cc.endFunction(context == Context.STATEMENT);
312 | 
313 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
314 |           add(")");
315 |         }
316 |         break;
317 | 
318 |       case Token.GET:
319 |       case Token.SET:
320 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getParent().getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT);
321 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
322 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
323 | 
324 |         // Get methods are unnamed
325 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getFirstChild().getString().isEmpty());
326 |         if (type == Token.GET) {
327 |           // Get methods have no parameters.
328 |           Preconditions.checkState(!first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasChildren());
329 |           add("get ");
330 |         } else {
331 |           // Set methods have one parameter.
332 |           Preconditions.checkState(first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasOneChild());
333 |           add("set ");
334 |         }
335 | 
336 |         // The name is on the GET or SET node.
337 |         String name = n.getString();
338 |         Node fn = first;
339 |         Node parameters = fn.getChildAtIndex(1);
340 |         Node body = fn.getLastChild();
341 | 
342 |         // Add the property name.
343 |         if (TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(name) &&
344 |             // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
345 |             // unicode escaped.
346 |             NodeUtil.isLatin(name)) {
347 |           add(name);
348 |         } else {
349 |           add(jsString(n.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder));
350 |         }
351 | 
352 |         add(parameters);
353 |         add(body, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
354 |         break;
355 | 
356 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
357 |       case Token.BLOCK: {
358 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
359 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
360 |         }
361 |         boolean preserveBlock = context == Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
362 |         if (preserveBlock) {
363 |           cc.beginBlock();
364 |         }
365 | 
366 |         boolean preferLineBreaks =
367 |             type == Token.SCRIPT ||
368 |             (type == Token.BLOCK &&
369 |                 !preserveBlock &&
370 |                 n.getParent() != null &&
371 |                 n.getParent().getType() == Token.SCRIPT);
372 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
373 |           add(c, Context.STATEMENT);
374 | 
375 |           // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions
376 |           if (c.getType() == Token.VAR) {
377 |             cc.endStatement();
378 |           }
379 | 
380 |           if (c.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
381 |             cc.maybeLineBreak();
382 |           }
383 | 
384 |           // Prefer to break lines in between top-level statements
385 |           // because top level statements are more homogeneous.
386 |           if (preferLineBreaks) {
387 |             cc.notePreferredLineBreak();
388 |           }
389 |         }
390 |         if (preserveBlock) {
391 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
392 |         }
393 |         break;
394 |       }
395 | 
396 |       case Token.FOR:
397 |         if (childCount == 4) {
398 |           add("for(");
399 |           if (first.getType() == Token.VAR) {
400 |             add(first, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
401 |           } else {
402 |             addExpr(first, 0, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
403 |           }
404 |           add(";");
405 |           add(first.getNext());
406 |           add(";");
407 |           add(first.getNext().getNext());
408 |           add(")");
409 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
410 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
411 |         } else {
412 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
413 |           add("for(");
414 |           add(first);
415 |           add("in");
416 |           add(first.getNext());
417 |           add(")");
418 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
419 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
420 |         }
421 |         break;
422 | 
423 |       case Token.DO:
424 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
425 |         add("do");
426 |         addNonEmptyStatement(first, Context.OTHER, false);
427 |         add("while(");
428 |         add(last);
429 |         add(")");
430 |         cc.endStatement();
431 |         break;
432 | 
433 |       case Token.WHILE:
434 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
435 |         add("while(");
436 |         add(first);
437 |         add(")");
438 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
439 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
440 |         break;
441 | 
442 |       case Token.EMPTY:
443 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
444 |         break;
445 | 
446 |       case Token.GETPROP: {
447 |         Preconditions.checkState(
448 |             childCount == 2,
449 |             "Bad GETPROP: expected 2 children, but got %s", childCount);
450 |         Preconditions.checkState(
451 |             last.getType() == Token.STRING,
452 |             "Bad GETPROP: RHS should be STRING");
453 |         boolean needsParens = (first.getType() == Token.NUMBER);
454 |         if (needsParens) {
455 |           add("(");
456 |         }
457 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
458 |         if (needsParens) {
459 |           add(")");
460 |         }
461 |         add(".");
462 |         addIdentifier(last.getString());
463 |         break;
464 |       }
465 | 
466 |       case Token.GETELEM:
467 |         Preconditions.checkState(
468 |             childCount == 2,
469 |             "Bad GETELEM: expected 2 children but got %s", childCount);
470 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
471 |         add("[");
472 |         add(first.getNext());
473 |         add("]");
474 |         break;
475 | 
476 |       case Token.WITH:
477 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
478 |         add("with(");
479 |         add(first);
480 |         add(")");
481 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
482 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
483 |         break;
484 | 
485 |       case Token.INC:
486 |       case Token.DEC: {
487 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
488 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
489 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP);
490 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
491 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
492 |         if (postProp != 0) {
493 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
494 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
495 |         } else {
496 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
497 |           add(first);
498 |         }
499 |         break;
500 |       }
501 | 
502 |       case Token.CALL:
503 |         // We have two special cases here:
504 |         // 1) If the left hand side of the call is a direct reference to eval,
505 |         // then it must have a DIRECT_EVAL annotation. If it does not, then
506 |         // that means it was originally an indirect call to eval, and that
507 |         // indirectness must be preserved.
508 |         // 2) If the left hand side of the call is a property reference,
509 |         // then the call must not a FREE_CALL annotation. If it does, then
510 |         // that means it was originally an call without an explicit this and
511 |         // that must be preserved.
512 |         if (isIndirectEval(first)
513 |             || n.getBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL) && NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
514 |           add("(0,");
515 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.COMMA));
516 |           add(")");
517 |         } else {
518 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
519 |         }
520 |         add("(");
521 |         addList(first.getNext());
522 |         add(")");
523 |         break;
524 | 
525 |       case Token.IF:
526 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
527 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
528 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
529 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
530 |           cc.beginBlock();
531 |         }
532 | 
533 |         add("if(");
534 |         add(first);
535 |         add(")");
536 | 
537 |         if (hasElse) {
538 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
539 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
540 |           add("else");
541 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
542 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
543 |         } else {
544 |           addNonEmptyStatement(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
545 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
546 |         }
547 | 
548 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
549 |           cc.endBlock();
550 |         }
551 |         break;
552 | 
553 |       case Token.NULL:
554 |       case Token.THIS:
555 |       case Token.FALSE:
556 |       case Token.TRUE:
557 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
558 |         add(Node.tokenToName(type));
559 |         break;
560 | 
561 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
562 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
563 |         add("continue");
564 |         if (childCount == 1) {
565 |           if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
566 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
567 |           }
568 |           add(" ");
569 |           add(first);
570 |         }
571 |         cc.endStatement();
572 |         break;
573 | 
574 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
575 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
576 |         add("debugger");
577 |         cc.endStatement();
578 |         break;
579 | 
580 |       case Token.BREAK:
581 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
582 |         add("break");
583 |         if (childCount == 1) {
584 |           if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
585 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
586 |           }
587 |           add(" ");
588 |           add(first);
589 |         }
590 |         cc.endStatement();
591 |         break;
592 | 
593 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
594 |         throw new Error("Unexpected EXPR_VOID. Should be EXPR_RESULT.");
595 | 
596 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
597 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
598 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
599 |         cc.endStatement();
600 |         break;
601 | 
602 |       case Token.NEW:
603 |         add("new ");
604 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
605 | 
606 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
607 |         // to force parentheses. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
608 |         // first viable parentheses (don't traverse into functions).
609 |         if (NodeUtil.containsType(first, Token.CALL, new MatchNotFunction())) {
610 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
611 |         }
612 |         addExpr(first, precedence);
613 | 
614 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
615 |         Node next = first.getNext();
616 |         if (next != null) {
617 |           add("(");
618 |           addList(next);
619 |           add(")");
620 |         }
621 |         break;
622 | 
623 |       case Token.STRING:
624 |         if (childCount !=
625 |             ((n.getParent() != null &&
626 |               n.getParent().getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) ? 1 : 0)) {
627 |           throw new IllegalStateException(
628 |               "Unexpected String children: " + n.getParent().toStringTree());
629 |         }
630 |         add(jsString(n.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder));
631 |         break;
632 | 
633 |       case Token.DELPROP:
634 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
635 |         add("delete ");
636 |         add(first);
637 |         break;
638 | 
639 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
640 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
641 |         if (needsParens) {
642 |           add("(");
643 |         }
644 |         add("{");
645 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
646 |           if (c != first) {
647 |             cc.listSeparator();
648 |           }
649 | 
650 |           if (c.getType() == Token.GET || c.getType() == Token.SET) {
651 |             add(c);
652 |           } else {
653 |             // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they are
654 |             // not JavaScript keywords
655 |             if (c.getType() == Token.STRING &&
656 |                 !c.isQuotedString() &&
657 |                 !TokenStream.isKeyword(c.getString()) &&
658 |                 TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(c.getString()) &&
659 |                 // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
660 |                 // unicode escaped.
661 |                 NodeUtil.isLatin(c.getString())) {
662 |               add(c.getString());
663 |             } else {
664 |               addExpr(c, 1);
665 |             }
666 |             add(":");
667 |             addExpr(c.getFirstChild(), 1);
668 |           }
669 |         }
670 |         add("}");
671 |         if (needsParens) {
672 |           add(")");
673 |         }
674 |         break;
675 |       }
676 | 
677 |       case Token.SWITCH:
678 |         add("switch(");
679 |         add(first);
680 |         add(")");
681 |         cc.beginBlock();
682 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
683 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
684 |         break;
685 | 
686 |       case Token.CASE:
687 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
688 |         add("case ");
689 |         add(first);
690 |         addCaseBody(last);
691 |         break;
692 | 
693 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
694 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
695 |         add("default");
696 |         addCaseBody(first);
697 |         break;
698 | 
699 |       case Token.LABEL:
700 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
701 |         if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
702 |           throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
703 |         }
704 |         add(first);
705 |         add(":");
706 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
707 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
708 |         break;
709 | 
710 |       // This node is auto generated in anonymous functions and should just get
711 |       // ignored for our purposes.
712 |       case Token.SETNAME:
713 |         break;
714 | 
715 |       default:
716 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
717 |     }
718 | 
719 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
720 |   }

===== M0449 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isEquivalentTo(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 632-658
Documentation:
/**
   * Two function types are equal if their signatures match. Since they don't
   * have signatures, two interfaces are equal if their names match.
   */
Method code:
632 |   @Override
633 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(JSType otherType) {
634 |     if (!(otherType instanceof FunctionType)) {
635 |       return false;
636 |     }
637 |     FunctionType that = (FunctionType) otherType;
638 |     if (!that.isFunctionType()) {
639 |       return false;
640 |     }
641 |     if (this.isConstructor()) {
642 |       if (that.isConstructor()) {
643 |         return this == that;
644 |       }
645 |       return false;
646 |     }
647 |     if (this.isInterface()) {
648 |       if (that.isInterface()) {
649 |         return this.getReferenceName().equals(that.getReferenceName());
650 |       }
651 |       return false;
652 |     }
653 |     if (that.isInterface()) {
654 |       return false;
655 |     }
656 |     return this.typeOfThis.isEquivalentTo(that.typeOfThis) &&
657 |         this.call.isEquivalentTo(that.call);
658 |   }

===== M0477 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DeadAssignmentsElimination.tryRemoveDeadAssignments(NodeTraversal,ControlFlowGraph<Node>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DeadAssignmentsElimination.java
Original range: 117-152
Documentation:
/**
   * Try to remove useless assignments from a control flow graph that has been
   * annotated with liveness information.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal.
   * @param cfg The control flow graph of the program annotated with liveness
   *        information.
   */
Method code:
117 |   private void tryRemoveDeadAssignments(NodeTraversal t,
118 |       ControlFlowGraph<Node> cfg) {
119 |     Iterable<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> nodes = cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes();
120 | 
121 |     for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> cfgNode : nodes) {
122 |       FlowState<LiveVariableLattice> state =
123 |           cfgNode.getAnnotation();
124 |       Node n = cfgNode.getValue();
125 |       if (n == null) {
126 |         continue;
127 |       }
128 |       switch (n.getType()) {
129 |         case Token.IF:
130 |         case Token.WHILE:
131 |         case Token.DO:
132 |           tryRemoveAssignment(t, NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(n), state);
133 |           continue;
134 |         case Token.FOR:
135 |           if (!NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
136 |             tryRemoveAssignment(
137 |                 t, NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(n), state);
138 |           }
139 |           continue;
140 |         case Token.SWITCH:
141 |         case Token.CASE:
142 |         case Token.RETURN:
143 |           if (n.hasChildren()) {
144 |             tryRemoveAssignment(t, n.getFirstChild(), state);
145 |           }
146 |           continue;
147 |         // TODO(user): case Token.VAR: Remove var a=1;a=2;.....
148 |       }
149 | 
150 |       tryRemoveAssignment(t, n, state);
151 |     }
152 |   }

===== M0478 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.PrototypeObjectType(JSTypeRegistry,String,ObjectType,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 108-120
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates an object type, allowing specification of the implicit prototype
   * when creating native objects.
   */
Method code:
108 |   PrototypeObjectType(JSTypeRegistry registry, String className,
109 |       ObjectType implicitPrototype, boolean nativeType) {
110 |     super(registry);
111 |     this.properties = Maps.newTreeMap();

===== M0494 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.getScope()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 555-569
Documentation:
/** Gets the current scope. */
Method code:
555 |   public Scope getScope() {
556 |     Scope scope = scopes.isEmpty() ? null : scopes.peek();
557 |     if (scopeRoots.isEmpty()) {
558 |       return scope;
559 |     }
560 | 
561 |     Iterator<Node> it = scopeRoots.descendingIterator();
562 |     while (it.hasNext()) {
563 |       scope = scopeCreator.createScope(it.next(), scope);
564 |       scopes.push(scope);
565 |     }
566 |     scopeRoots.clear();
567 | 
568 |     return scope;
569 |   }

===== M0509 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLatin(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2401-2411
Documentation:
/** Test if all characters in the string are in the Basic Latin (aka ASCII)
   * character set - that they have UTF-16 values equal to or below 0x7f.
   * This check can find which identifiers with Unicode characters need to be
   * escaped in order to allow resulting files to be processed by non-Unicode
   * aware UNIX tools and editors.
   * *
   * See http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_characters_in_Unicode
   * for more on Basic Latin.
   *
   * @param s The string to be checked for ASCII-goodness.
   *
   * @return True if all characters in the string are in Basic Latin set.
   */
Method code:
2401 |   static boolean isLatin(String s) {
2402 |     char LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN = 0x7f;
2403 |     int len = s.length();
2404 |     for (int index = 0; index < len; index++) {
2405 |       char c = s.charAt(index);
2406 |       if (c > LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN) {
2407 |         return false;
2408 |       }
2409 |     }
2410 |     return true;
2411 |   }

1070 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1071 |     // individual file parse trees.
1072 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1073 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1074 |     }
1075 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1076 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1077 |     }
1078 | 
1079 |     // Parse main js sources.
1080 |     jsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1081 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1082 | 
1083 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1084 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot,
1085 |           options.tracer == TracerMode.ALL);
1086 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1087 |     }
1088 | 
1089 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
1090 | 
1091 |     try {
1092 |       // Parse externs sources.
1093 |       externsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1094 |       externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1095 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1096 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1097 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1098 |           return null;
1099 |         }
1100 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1101 |       }
1102 | 
1103 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1104 |       if (options.manageClosureDependencies) {
1105 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1106 |           input.setCompiler(this);
1107 | 
1108 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1109 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1110 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1111 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1112 |           }
1113 |         }
1114 | 
1115 |         try {
1116 |           inputs =
1117 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1118 |               .manageDependencies(
1119 |                   options.manageClosureDependenciesEntryPoints, inputs);
1120 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1121 |           report(JSError.make(
1122 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1123 |           return null;
1124 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1125 |           report(JSError.make(
1126 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1127 |           return null;
1128 |         }
1129 |       }
1130 | 
1131 |       // Check if inputs need to be rebuilt from modules.
1132 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1133 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1134 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1135 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1136 |           return null;
1137 |         }
1138 | 
1139 |         // Inputs can have a null AST during initial parse.
1140 |         if (n == null) {
1141 |           continue;
1142 |         }
1143 | 
1144 |         if (n.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
1145 |           JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1146 |           if (info.isExterns()) {
1147 |             // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1148 |             // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1149 |             // the externs pile anyways.
1150 |             externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1151 |             input.setIsExtern(true);
1152 | 
1153 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1154 | 
1155 |             externs.add(input);
1156 |             staleInputs = true;
1157 |           } else if (info.isNoCompile()) {
1158 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1159 |             staleInputs = true;
1160 |           }
1161 |         }
1162 |       }
1163 | 
1164 |       if (staleInputs) {
1165 |         fillEmptyModules(modules);
1166 |         rebuildInputsFromModules();
1167 |       }
1168 | 
1169 |       // Build the AST.
1170 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1171 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1172 |         if (n == null) {
1173 |           continue;
1174 |         }
1175 | 
1176 |         if (devMode) {
1177 |           runSanityCheck();
1178 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1179 |             return null;
1180 |           }
1181 |         }
1182 | 
1183 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1184 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1185 | 
1186 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1187 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1188 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1189 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1190 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1191 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1192 |         }
1193 | 
1194 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1195 |       }
1196 | 
1197 |       externAndJsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK, externsRoot, jsRoot);
1198 |       externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1199 | 
1200 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1201 |     } finally {
1202 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1203 |     }
1204 |   }

694 |   public void replaceChild(Node child, Node newChild) {
695 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(newChild.next == null,
696 |         "The new child node has siblings.");

710 |     }
711 |     if (child == last)

713 |     child.next = null;
714 |     child.parent = null;

===== M0552 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 607-619
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
607 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
608 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
609 |     if (typeSet == null) {
610 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this);
611 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
612 |     }
613 | 
614 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
615 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
616 | 
617 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
618 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
619 |   }

234 |     private void annotateFunctions(Node n, Node parent) {
235 |       JSDocInfo fnInfo = NodeUtil.getFunctionInfo(n);
236 | 
237 |       // Compute which function parameters are optional and
238 |       // which are var_args.
239 |       Node args = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
240 |       for (Node arg = args.getFirstChild();
241 |            arg != null;
242 |            arg = arg.getNext()) {
243 |         String argName = arg.getString();
244 |         JSTypeExpression typeExpr = fnInfo == null ?
245 |             null : fnInfo.getParameterType(argName);
246 | 
247 |         if (convention.isOptionalParameter(arg) ||
248 |             typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isOptionalArg()) {
249 |           arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_OPTIONAL_PARAM, true);
250 |         }
251 |         if (convention.isVarArgsParameter(arg) ||
252 |             typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isVarArgs()) {
253 |           arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_VAR_ARGS_PARAM, true);
254 |         }
255 |       }
256 |     }

Original range: 175-191
Documentation:
/**
     * There are two types of calls we are interested in calls without explicit
     * "this" values (what we are call "free" calls) and direct call to eval.
     */
Method code:
175 |     private void annotateCalls(Node n) {
176 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.CALL);
177 | 
178 |       // Keep track of of the "this" context of a call.  A call without an
179 |       // explicit "this" is a free call.
180 |       Node first = n.getFirstChild();
181 |       if (!NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
182 |         n.putBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL, true);
183 |       }
184 | 
185 |       // Keep track of the context in which eval is called. It is important
186 |       // to distinguish between "(0, eval)()" and "eval()".
187 |       if (first.getType() == Token.NAME &&
188 |           "eval".equals(first.getString())) {
189 |         first.putBooleanProp(Node.DIRECT_EVAL, true);
190 |       }
191 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 406-433
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
406 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
407 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
408 | 
409 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
410 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
411 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
412 |       return 0;
413 |     }
414 | 
415 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
416 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
417 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
418 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
419 |       // have been added
420 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
421 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
422 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
423 |       }
424 |     }
425 | 
426 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
427 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
428 |       return 0;
429 |     }
430 | 
431 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
432 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
433 |   }

===== M0563 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addNumber(double)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 233-259
Documentation:

Method code:
233 |   void addNumber(double x) {
234 |     // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of x- -4 as
235 |     // x--4 (which is a syntax error).
236 |     char prev = getLastChar();
237 |     if (x < 0 && prev == '-') {
238 |       add(" ");
239 |     }
240 | 
241 |     if ((long) x == x) {
242 |       long value = (long) x;
243 |       long mantissa = value;
244 |       int exp = 0;
245 |       if (Math.abs(x) >= 100) {
246 |         while (mantissa / 10 * Math.pow(10, exp + 1) == value) {
247 |           mantissa /= 10;
248 |           exp++;
249 |         }
250 |       }
251 |       if (exp > 2) {
252 |         add(Long.toString(mantissa) + "E" + Integer.toString(exp));
253 |       } else {
254 |         add(Long.toString(value));
255 |       }
256 |     } else {
257 |       add(String.valueOf(x));
258 |     }
259 |   }

===== M0564 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.flow(DiGraphNode<N, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 267-278
Documentation:
/**
   * Performs a single flow through a node.
   *
   * @return {@code true} if the flow state differs from the previous state.
   */
Method code:
267 |   protected boolean flow(DiGraphNode<N, Branch> node) {
268 |     FlowState<L> state = node.getAnnotation();
269 |     if (isForward()) {
270 |       L outBefore = state.out;
271 |       state.out = flowThrough(node.getValue(), state.in);
272 |       return !outBefore.equals(state.out);
273 |     } else {
274 |       L inBefore = state.in;
275 |       state.in = flowThrough(node.getValue(), state.out);
276 |       return !inBefore.equals(state.in);
277 |     }
278 |   }

185 |   private boolean isConnectedInDirection(N n1, Predicate<E> edgeMatcher, N n2) {
186 |     // Verify the nodes.
187 |     DiGraphNode<N, E> dNode1 = getNodeOrFail(n1);

===== M0590 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 117-170
Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
117 |   private void scanVars(Node n, Node parent) {
118 |     switch (n.getType()) {
119 |       case Token.VAR:
120 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
121 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
122 |              child != null;) {
123 |           Node next = child.getNext();
124 |           declareVar(child);
125 |           child = next;
126 |         }
127 |         return;
128 | 
129 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
130 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
131 |           return;
132 |         }
133 | 
134 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
135 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
136 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
137 |           return;
138 |         }
139 |         declareVar(n.getFirstChild());
140 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
141 | 
142 |       case Token.CATCH:
143 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
144 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NAME);
145 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
146 |         // is the code block
147 | 
148 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
149 |         final Node block = var.getNext();
150 | 
151 |         declareVar(var);
152 |         scanVars(block, n);
153 |         return;  // only one child to scan
154 | 
155 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
156 |         sourceName = (String) n.getProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP);
157 |         break;
158 |     }
159 | 
160 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
161 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
162 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
163 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
164 |            child != null;) {
165 |         Node next = child.getNext();
166 |         scanVars(child, n);
167 |         child = next;
168 |       }
169 |     }
170 |   }

Method code:
255 |   private void setSourceInfo(Node irNode, AstNode node) {
256 |     // If we have a named function, set the position to that of the name.
257 |     if (irNode.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
258 |         irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno() != -1) {
259 |       irNode.setLineno(irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno());
260 |       irNode.setCharno(irNode.getFirstChild().getCharno());
261 |     } else {
262 |       if (irNode.getLineno() == -1) {
263 |         // If we didn't already set the line, then set it now.  This avoids
264 |         // cases like ParenthesizedExpression where we just return a previous
265 |         // node, but don't want the new node to get its parent's line number.
266 |         int lineno = node.getLineno();
267 |         irNode.setLineno(lineno);
268 |         int charno = position2charno(node.getAbsolutePosition());
269 |         irNode.setCharno(charno);
270 |       }
271 |     }
272 |   }

/*
   *  0 comma ,
   *  1 assignment = += -= *= /= %= <<= >>= >>>= &= ^= |=
   *  2 conditional ?:
   *  3 logical-or ||
   *  4 logical-and &&
   *  5 bitwise-or |
   *  6 bitwise-xor ^
   *  7 bitwise-and &
   *  8 equality == !=
   *  9 relational < <= > >=
   * 10 bitwise shift << >> >>>
   * 11 addition/subtraction + -
   * 12 multiply/divide * / %
   * 13 negation/increment ! ~ - ++ --
   * 14 call, member () [] .
   */
Method code:
1076 |   static int precedence(int type) {
1077 |     switch (type) {
1078 |       case Token.COMMA:  return 0;
1079 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
1080 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
1081 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
1082 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
1083 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
1084 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
1085 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
1086 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
1087 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
1088 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
1089 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
1090 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return 1;
1091 |       case Token.HOOK:   return 2;  // ?: operator
1092 |       case Token.OR:     return 3;
1093 |       case Token.AND:    return 4;
1094 |       case Token.BITOR:  return 5;
1095 |       case Token.BITXOR: return 6;
1096 |       case Token.BITAND: return 7;
1097 |       case Token.EQ:
1098 |       case Token.NE:
1099 |       case Token.SHEQ:
1100 |       case Token.SHNE:   return 8;
1101 |       case Token.LT:
1102 |       case Token.GT:
1103 |       case Token.LE:
1104 |       case Token.GE:
1105 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
1106 |       case Token.IN:     return 9;
1107 |       case Token.LSH:
1108 |       case Token.RSH:
1109 |       case Token.URSH:   return 10;
1110 |       case Token.SUB:
1111 |       case Token.ADD:    return 11;
1112 |       case Token.MUL:
1113 |       case Token.MOD:
1114 |       case Token.DIV:    return 12;
1115 |       case Token.INC:
1116 |       case Token.DEC:
1117 |       case Token.NEW:
1118 |       case Token.DELPROP:
1119 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
1120 |       case Token.VOID:
1121 |       case Token.NOT:
1122 |       case Token.BITNOT:
1123 |       case Token.POS:
1124 |       case Token.NEG:    return 13;
1125 | 
1126 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
1127 |       case Token.CALL:
1128 |       case Token.EMPTY:
1129 |       case Token.FALSE:
1130 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1131 |       case Token.GETELEM:
1132 |       case Token.GETPROP:
1133 |       case Token.GET_REF:
1134 |       case Token.IF:
1135 |       case Token.LP:
1136 |       case Token.NAME:
1137 |       case Token.NULL:
1138 |       case Token.NUMBER:

1140 |       case Token.REGEXP:
1141 |       case Token.RETURN:
1142 |       case Token.STRING:
1143 |       case Token.THIS:
1144 |       case Token.TRUE:
1145 |         return 15;
1146 | 
1147 |       default: throw new Error("Unknown precedence for " +
1148 |                                Node.tokenToName(type) +
1149 |                                " (type " + type + ")");
1150 |     }

===== M0617 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.toString(StringBuilder,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 923-1039
Documentation:

Method code:
923 |   private void toString(
924 |       StringBuilder sb,
925 |       boolean printSource,
926 |       boolean printAnnotations,
927 |       boolean printType) {
928 |     if (Token.printTrees) {
929 |       sb.append(Token.name(type));
930 |       if (this instanceof StringNode) {
931 |         sb.append(' ');
932 |         sb.append(getString());
933 |       } else if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
934 |         sb.append(' ');
935 |         // In the case of JsDoc trees, the first child is often not a string
936 |         // which causes exceptions to be thrown when calling toString or
937 |         // toStringTree.
938 |         if (first.getType() == Token.STRING) {
939 |           sb.append(first.getString());
940 |         }
941 |       } else if (this instanceof ScriptOrFnNode) {
942 |         ScriptOrFnNode sof = (ScriptOrFnNode) this;
943 |         if (this instanceof FunctionNode) {
944 |           FunctionNode fn = (FunctionNode) this;
945 |           sb.append(' ');
946 |           sb.append(fn.getFunctionName());
947 |         }
948 |         if (printSource) {
949 |           sb.append(" [source name: ");
950 |           sb.append(sof.getSourceName());
951 |           sb.append("] [encoded source length: ");
952 |           sb.append(sof.getEncodedSourceEnd() - sof.getEncodedSourceStart());
953 |           sb.append("] [base line: ");
954 |           sb.append(sof.getBaseLineno());
955 |           sb.append("] [end line: ");
956 |           sb.append(sof.getEndLineno());
957 |           sb.append(']');
958 |         }
959 |       } else if (type == Token.NUMBER) {
960 |         sb.append(' ');
961 |         sb.append(getDouble());
962 |       }
963 |       if (printSource) {
964 |         int lineno = getLineno();
965 |         if (lineno != -1) {
966 |           sb.append(' ');
967 |           sb.append(lineno);
968 |         }
969 |       }
970 | 
971 |       if (printAnnotations) {
972 |         int[] keys = getSortedPropTypes();
973 |         for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
974 |           int type = keys[i];
975 |           PropListItem x = lookupProperty(type);
976 |           sb.append(" [");
977 |           sb.append(propToString(type));
978 |           sb.append(": ");
979 |           String value;
980 |           switch (type) {
981 |             case TARGETBLOCK_PROP: // can't add this as it recurses
982 |               value = "target block property";
983 |               break;
984 |             case LOCAL_BLOCK_PROP: // can't add this as it is dull
985 |               value = "last local block";
986 |               break;
987 |             case ISNUMBER_PROP:
988 |               switch (x.intValue) {
989 |                 case BOTH:
990 |                   value = "both";
991 |                   break;
992 |                 case RIGHT:
993 |                   value = "right";
994 |                   break;
995 |                 case LEFT:
996 |                   value = "left";
997 |                   break;
998 |                 default:
999 |                   throw Kit.codeBug();
1000 |               }
1001 |               break;
1002 |             case SPECIALCALL_PROP:
1003 |               switch (x.intValue) {
1004 |                 case SPECIALCALL_EVAL:
1005 |                   value = "eval";
1006 |                   break;
1007 |                 case SPECIALCALL_WITH:
1008 |                   value = "with";
1009 |                   break;
1010 |                 default:
1011 |                   // NON_SPECIALCALL should not be stored
1012 |                   throw Kit.codeBug();
1013 |               }
1014 |               break;
1015 |             default:
1016 |               Object obj = x.objectValue;
1017 |               if (obj != null) {
1018 |                 value = obj.toString();
1019 |               } else {
1020 |                 value = String.valueOf(x.intValue);
1021 |               }
1022 |               break;
1023 |           }
1024 |           sb.append(value);
1025 |           sb.append(']');
1026 |         }
1027 |       }
1028 | 
1029 |       if (printType) {
1030 |         if (jsType != null) {
1031 |           String jsTypeString = jsType.toString();
1032 |           if (jsTypeString != null) {
1033 |             sb.append(" : ");
1034 |             sb.append(jsTypeString);
1035 |           }
1036 |         }
1037 |       }
1038 |     }
1039 |   }

===== M0619 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionParamBuilder.addVarArgs(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionParamBuilder.java
Original range: 94-109
Documentation:
/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.
103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.
104 |     if (!type.isEmptyType()) {
105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;
109 |   }
